purpose = "- 将模型预训练阶段已掌握的文件操作转化为原生记忆接口，可降低教师指导与学生探索之间的接口错配。\n- 表明持久化工作空间能够支持跨上下文的记忆写入、修订、检索与复用，适合长程智能体任务。\n- 为编码智能体训练提供可扩展方案：使用冻结教师和异步在策略蒸馏学习记忆控制，而非额外设计专用记忆协议。\n- 在外部终端与软件工程基准上的提升说明，文件式记忆策略具备一定跨环境泛化能力。"
notes = "标题：Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II: Unlocking the Memory Potential of Pre-trained File Operations for Long-Horizon Tasks via On-Policy Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14009\n摘要：Language-model agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks whose interaction histories exceed the model's active context. They must therefore decide what to write down and what to read back, and when to do either. Recent work has begun to use on-policy distillation (OPD) to make memory control part of the policy, relying on memory tools and compaction protocols that lie outside either model's pre-training. However, this weakens the distillation on both sides: the teacher cannot give useful guidance on an interface it has never used, and the student samples too few meaningful memory actions for that guidance to take effect. To study this problem, we introduce Coding Agent Memory Gym II (CAMG-II), a suite of long-horizon agentic-RL environments spanning Shop, Coding, AutoResearch, General,"

A leaderboard of 13 systems gives 61.2% for the English-root checkpoint, 77.3% for the shipped multilingual one and 96.6% for the best fine-tun"

summary = "Omni-JEV联合建模文本及角色区分的双流语音，在全双工对话中预测回复、保持、打断与恢复等五类交互动作。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13777"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "Omni-JEV将全双工对话决策定义在采集语音片段的端点，根据当前输入、助手响应状态和交互历史输出五种动作的概率。系统使用对齐的语音到语音理解骨干分别编码用户与助手参考语音，并与文本输入共享候选动作读出层。训练流程先利用双语弱监督进行交叉熵微调，再通过面向概率敏感决策的RLCD强化学习优化策略。实验比较Gaussian-logit与categorical Choice RLCD，结果显示两者在分类准确率和概率质量之间存在不同权衡，其中Gaussian Omni-JEV的音频与文本测试准确率分别达到84.08%和96.55%。"
purpose = "- 为全双工语音助手提供统一动作接口，决定何时回复、保持倾听、打断或恢复响应。\n- 展示角色分离的双流声学编码对处理重叠语音和交互状态的重要性。\n- 说明语音交互策略不仅需要分类准确率，还应关注动作概率的校准质量。\n- 可用于低延迟语音助手、实时对话系统和主动式智能的响应时机优化。"
notes = "标题：Omni-JEV: Text and Dual-Stream Speech Decisions\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13777\n摘要：A user utterance does not uniquely determine the next action in full-duplex dialogue: its consequences depend on the assistant's response state and interaction history. Speech activity, utterance completion, and interruption intent provide related evidence, whose implications for a response require an explicit action policy. We formulate this task at collected-segment endpoints and introduce Omni-JEV, which maps text or role-indexed user and assistant-reference speech to probabilities over five interaction actions. An aligned speech-to-speech understanding backbone encodes the acoustic roles separately and shares its candidate readout with text inputs. Bilingual weak supervision supports cross-entropy fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning for probability-sensitive decisions (RLCD)"

notes = "标题：TRUER: Test-Time Reward Correction Using an Error-Aware Rectifier for Reinforcement Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13714\n摘要：Test-time reinforcement learning enables language models to adapt using rewards derived from their own answers. However, majority voting can reinforce incorrect solutions even when correct alternatives are available. Our training audit shows that false positives (incorrect responses receiving positive rewards) dominate reward errors. We focus on a practical regime where ground-truth rewards are difficult to obtain at scale, while a limited labeled source set is available. We introduce TRUER, a framework that uses limited supervision to train a reward rectifier that distinguishes correctness from consensus. To achieve this goal, we train the rectifier with an emphasis on incorrect majorities and correct minorities, targeting cases where consensus and correctness disagree. After that, we fre"

summary = "BOPD 通过先将特权参考监督内化到中间模型、再蒸馏至全新学生模型，缓解持续在策略自蒸馏中的师生分布失配与性能退化。"

code = ""

content = "在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）使用带参考条件的模型副本充当教师，初期能够有效改善学生模型生成轨迹，但持续训练后可能出现显著性能退化。研究将这一现象归因于持续演化的无参考学生与冻结的特权教师之间不断扩大的分布失配，使教师逐渐产生误导性监督。作者提出两阶段的自举式在策略蒸馏（BOPD）：先借助 OPSD 将任务相关信息内化到中间模型，再由该无参考中间模型蒸馏一个从初始权重重新开始的学生模型。跨模型实验表明，BOPD 在数学推理和代码生成上均超过初始模型及所构造的 OPSD 教师。"

In the early stage of OPSD, the privileged teacher remains closely aligned with the student and provides useful guidance for improving the student's generation trajectories. However, as t"

id = "topoverus"
title = "TopoVerus：基于学习反例与课程奖励的形式化证明修复"
subtopic = "程序修复"
topics = [
    "形式化验证",
    "软件缺陷修复",
    "强化学习",
    "过程验证",
    "人工智能辅助编程",
]
research = [

    "AgentTrainEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "形式化验证",
    "软件缺陷修复",
    "强化学习",
    "反例生成",
    "课程学习",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "TopoVerus通过学习单次生成结构化反例、修复依据与安全证明，并以拓扑课程奖励引导不变量修复顺序，显著提升Verus形式化证明修复成功率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13627"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "TopoVerus将反例生成从独立验证任务转化为数据驱动的诊断目标，使模型能够在单次生成中输出源代码级结构化反例、修复依据和修复后的Verus证明。框架利用Verus验证反馈进行强化学习，并设置证明代码安全门，确保生成结果安全且能够通过验证器。其拓扑图课程奖励根据归纳性测试得到的不变量依赖关系，引导模型按照合理的拓扑顺序修复不变量。在包含471道题的七个Verus基准上，TopoVerus取得61.6%的Safe-Pass@1和72.2%的Safe-Pass@3，分别超过Claude Sonnet 4.5基线5.7和6.3个百分点；学习到的反例还能将其他证明修复方法的成功率相对思维链诊断提升64.3%。"
purpose = "- 将反例引导诊断内化为模型能力，可减少推理阶段反复调用验证器和迭代生成反例的成本。\n- 利用不变量依赖图安排修复课程，为多步骤形式化证明修复提供更有效的信用分配与训练顺序。\n- 结构化反例可作为可复用的诊断信息，增强现有证明修复系统的推理与错误定位能力。\n- 安全门与Safe-Pass指标为评估生成证明的功能正确性和代码安全性提供了可借鉴方案。"
notes = "标题：Curriculum proof repair with learned counterexamples\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13627\n摘要：Language models (LMs) have substantially lowered the barrier to applying formal verification for building reliable and secure software. Existing approaches commonly follow the verifier-in-the-loop paradigm, but the verifier often provides only coarse, binary pass/fail feedback, which offers little actionable signal on how the proof should be repaired. Recent work shows that counterexamples provide more actionable feedback by grounding proof failures in concrete program states, but generating useful ones at inference time requires costly iterative refinement. We present TopoVerus, an LM-based framework for Verus proof repair that internalizes counterexample-guided diagnosis as a model capability, producing useful counterexamples in a single pass. Our key insight is to train counterexample g"


[[items]]
id = "synapse-control"
title = "Synapse-Control：以轻量运行时控制激活智能体经验"
subtopic = "经验学习"
topics = [
    "智能体",
    "经验复用",
    "智能体运行时",
    "系统优化",
    "成本优化",
]
research = [
    "AgentEvolve",
    "SystemEvolve",
    "MemoryEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "经验复用",
    "智能体运行时",
    "工具调用",
    "成本优化",
    "状态追踪",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Synapse-Control通过规则筛选、1.7B分类器、微契约和提示价值表，在减少生成式诊断开销的同时提升工具型智能体的经验复用效果。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13615"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "Synapse-Control为工具型智能体引入轻量控制平面，负责选择历史建议并判断何时将其发送给任务执行模型。系统先用规则筛选相关建议，再由基于教师标注状态—建议对微调的1.7B分类器完成候选评分，从而替代昂贵的生成式故障诊断。微契约用于编码必需参数、合法选项等可复用接口检查，Prompt Value Table则结合历史反馈、冷却机制和词元预算抑制建议的重复发送。在AppWorld和WebShop上，每种方法共进行1,308次尝试，Synapse-Control取得270次成功，较Base提升29.8%，替代4,987次生成式诊断调用，并将相对Sentry的辅助控制时间分别降低50.2%和51.0%。"
purpose = "- 为智能体经验库提供低成本的运行时检索与建议触发机制，避免每一步都调用大型模型进行故障诊断。\n- 可将工具接口约束封装为微契约，在不规定完整任务程序的情况下提升工具调用可靠性。\n- 提示价值表可用于控制重复建议和词元消耗，为长程任务中的经验复用与成本治理提供参考。\n- 说明小型分类器与规则控制可以承担部分元决策工作，为大小模型协同的智能体运行时设计提供实践依据。"
notes = "标题：Synapse-Control: Making Agent Experience Actionable with Lightweight Runtime Control\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13615\n摘要：Large language model agents reuse execution experience to improve reliability on complex tool-based tasks. Systems such as Sentry call large models to generate failure diagnoses before retrieving advice, adding inference cost to experience reuse. We present Synapse-Control, an agent runtime whose lightweight control plane selects advice and determines when to send it to the task-executing model. Rules shortlist relevant advice, and a lightweight 1.7B classifier fine-tuned on teacher-labeled state-suggestion pairs scores those candidates instead of generating a diagnosis. Micro-contracts encode reusable interface checks, such as required arguments or valid options, without prescribing complete task programs. A Prompt Value Table records earlier guidance and feedback to limit repeated sugges"


[[items]]
id = "grpo-token"
title = "GRPO 中稠密奖励值得其 Token 成本吗？"
subtopic = "过程奖励模型"
topics = [
    "强化学习",
    "奖励建模",
    "成本优化",
    "过程评估",
    "模型评估",
]
research = [

    "EvolveLRM",
]
keywords = [
    "GRPO",
    "RLVR",
    "过程奖励模型",
    "稠密奖励",
    "计算资源约束",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "在等生成 Token 与等 GPU 训练时间的计算预算下，蒙特卡洛稠密过程奖励不及标准 GRPO，而基于预测成功率的提示样本调度可带来小幅稳定增益。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13560"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出匹配计算量的评估协议，要求不同训练干预使用相同的生成 Token 预算和 GPU 训练时长，并通过预注册实验控制副作用。在六个模型的 1,668 次运行中，蒙特卡洛过程奖励的续写采样占生成 Token 的 91%，其按更新次数观察到的优势在等计算量比较下发生逆转。Qwen2.5-1.5B 上，该奖励在等 Token 和等训练时间条件下分别落后标准 GRPO 14.3 和 7.8 个百分点，四种稠密奖励变体均未胜出。相比之下，优先训练预测成功率接近 1/2 的提示可稳定提升准确率，但提高标准 GRPO 的初始学习率也能逼近这一收益。"
purpose = "- 评估强化学习训练干预时，应同时匹配生成 Token 数与 GPU 训练时间，避免按更新次数比较造成误判。\n- 稠密过程奖励的信用分配收益可能被续写采样成本抵消，部署前需核算完整计算开销。\n- 基于预测成功率的样本调度可能比复杂奖励设计更具计算效率，可优先选择略偏困难的训练提示。\n- 应将学习率等简单基线纳入对照，确认增益并非可由常规超参数调整轻易复现。"
notes = "标题：Are Dense Rewards Worth Their Tokens in GRPO?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13560\n摘要：RLVR trains language models to reason by rewarding correct final answers. A widely used algorithm, group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), gives every token of a solution the same credit, so dense process rewards have been proposed to credit intermediate steps, for example with a Monte-Carlo reward that scores each step by sampling continuations from it. Comparing such a reward with GRPO per training update, or vetting it with a gradient test on a single batch, leaves out what it costs: in our setting, the continuations alone take 91% of all generated tokens. We introduce a matched-compute protocol that gives every training intervention the same budget, measured in generated tokens and training GPU-hours, and tests it in preregistered studies with controls for side effects. Across 1,668"


[[items]]
id = "item-80"
title = "后训练如何影响语言模型的显式算法推理"
subtopic = "思维链"
topics = [
    "模型训练",
    "推理",
    "后训练",
    "数学人工智能",
    "人工智能可解释性",
]
research = [

    "LogicEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "思维链",
    "RLVR",
    "偏好优化",
    "表征探测",
    "数学推理",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "通过分析多位数加法中的进位状态，研究发现RLVR并未显著增强内部信息的可控性，却提高了显式思维链的跨检查点有效性及模型对关键推理内容的敏感度。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13552"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究依次分析 Olmo-3-7B-Instruct 的 SFT、DPO 与 RLVR 检查点，并以多位数加法的进位值作为已知中间状态。探针从 SFT 和 RLVR 模型内部激活中恢复进位信息的准确率相近，但干预这些信息无法可靠控制最终输出。相比之下，RLVR 生成的思维链被不同训练阶段的检查点采用时，均带来更高的答题准确率。RLVR 和 DPO 都会提升模型对进位相关语句的敏感度，但 DPO 未获得相应的性能增益，说明内容敏感性不等同于有效推理能力。"
purpose = "- 揭示后训练可能主要改善显式推理文本的可用性，而非简单增强内部中间状态的可解码性。\n- 提醒研究者不要将线性探针可恢复的信息直接解释为模型输出所依赖的因果表征。\n- 为比较 SFT、DPO 与 RLVR 对思维链忠实性和算法推理能力的影响提供实验范式。\n- 表明评估推理训练时应同时测量内容敏感性、跨模型可迁移性与最终任务准确率。"
notes = "标题：How Post-Training Influences Verbalized Algorithmic Computation in Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13552\n摘要：Post-training improves language-model reasoning, but how it changes the use of internal information and verbalized chain of thought (CoT) remains unclear. We study successive SFT, DPO, and RLVR checkpoints of Olmo-3-7B-Instruct on multi-digit addition, using carry values as known intermediate state. Probes recover carry information from internal activations with similar accuracy in SFT and RLVR, although manipulating this information does not reliably control model outputs. In contrast, the usefulness of verbalized reasoning differs: CoTs produced by RLVR yield higher accuracy across receiving checkpoints than those produced by SFT or DPO. We also show that carry-related statements receive higher importance scores after RLVR than matched random controls. DPO shows similar sensitivity to ca"


[[items]]
id = "item-81"
title = "智能体基准在任务耗尽前已失去排名能力"
subtopic = "评测审计"
topics = [
    "智能体评估",
    "模型评估",
    "测量效度",
    "评估透明度",
    "成本优化",
]
research = [
    "EvalEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "智能体评估",
    "测量效度",
    "动态基准测试",
    "评估透明度",
    "成本优化",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "对八项智能体基准的重评分表明，排名饱和主要源于二元通过率过粗且受参考答案上限约束，而非任务本身已被完全解决。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13541"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "作者重新分析了 MLE-bench、ALE-Bench、SWE-efficiency、AlgoTune、SWE-bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench 等八项基准的已发布运行结果。多数基准将一次运行压缩为通过或失败的一比特结果，导致头部智能体之间的差异小于测试误差，局部排名容易因测试修正或随机删减通过样本而改变。二元评分还受参考水平封顶：ALE-Bench 中按竞赛分数可区分的前十名，在以中位数人类表现为门槛时出现并列，即使近半运行实际超过该人类基准。论文提出采用带时间锚点的截断对数比评分给予分级得分，并将成本作为独立评估轴；Terminal-Bench 重跑显示，通过率相同的模型在被测试丢弃的连续指标上可相差十倍以上。"
purpose = "- 提醒基准设计者在增加更难任务前，先审计评分粒度、测试误差和参考上限造成的测量饱和。\n- 可用连续、分级得分替代单一通过率，以提升头部智能体排名的区分度和稳定性。\n- 将任务质量与运行成本分轴报告，避免单一总分掩盖性能—成本权衡。\n- 评测发布时应同步披露排名不确定性、时间锚点及重评分规则，以增强结果的可解释性与可复现性。"
notes = "标题：Agent Benchmarks Stop Ranking Before They Run Out of Tasks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13541\n摘要：Data-science and ML-engineering agents are evaluated on benchmarks such as MLE-bench, ALE-Bench, SWE-fficiency and AlgoTune, whose tasks yield a quantity (a Kaggle metric, contest score or speedup). When one stops separating the best models, the usual remedy is a harder version. Re-scoring released runs of eight benchmarks, including SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench for breadth, we find much of this saturation lies in the measurement, not the tasks. Most agent benchmarks reduce a run to one pass/fail bit, which fails in two ways. It is too coarse: neighbouring top-ten agents on SWE-bench Verified differ by less than its tests' errors, and correcting them reorders neighbours no more than deleting a random sixth of each agent's passes. It is capped at the reference: ALE-Bench's top ten,"


[[items]]
id = "rsi-tax"
title = "RSI Tax：递归自我改进何时得不偿失"
subtopic = "递归自我改进"
topics = [
    "递归自我改进",
    "成本优化",

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文提出将自我改进产生的完整词元开销纳入端到端评估，发现多种复杂 RSI 方法的性能收益可能不足以抵消其高昂成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13524"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文将“RSI Tax”定义为智能体自我改进过程消耗的词元成本，并与任务执行成本共同纳入持续任务流评估。实验覆盖六种方法和两个语言模型，且每项任务均在其反馈用于后续更新前完成评分。有限容量的笔记基线在准确率上持平或超过 Meta-Harness、EvoSkill 与 RRSI，同时平均节省 65.6%–71.0% 的总词元。成本分解表明，验证环节占三种 RSI 方法总体 RSI Tax 的 74.8%–93.6%，且大量生成的状态或产物在后续任务中未被使用。"
purpose = "- 为递归自我改进系统提供同时衡量能力收益与完整计算成本的端到端评估方式。\n- 提醒研究者不能仅比较部署后性能，还应计入探索、验证和写入等自我改进开销。\n- 支持采用有界笔记等简单基线检验复杂 RSI 流程是否真正具有成本效益。\n- 启发未来联合优化 RSI Tax、预期任务执行成本与持续能力增长。"
notes = "标题：The RSI Tax: When Recursive Self-Improvement Is Not Worth the Cost\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13524\n摘要：Recursive self-improvement (RSI) seeks to improve an agent using its own task experience. Its practical value depends on whether performance gains justify the full cost of self-improvement and task execution. We evaluate this trade-off on continual task streams, scoring each task before its feedback is used for subsequent updates. We define the \\emph{RSI Tax} as the token cost of the self-improvement process and include it alongside task-execution costs in end-to-end evaluation. Across six methods and two language models, a bounded-note baseline matches or exceeds the observed accuracy of Meta-Harness, EvoSkill, and RRSI, with mean total-token savings of 65.6-71.0\\%. We decompose the RSI Tax into verification, exploration, and writing costs, and track subsequent use of the associated state"

id = "deep-personalization"
title = "Deep Personalization：面向本地主动智能体的持续上下文观察"

topics = [

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Coco 通过持续观察桌面活动、维护紧凑的长期用户上下文并利用隐式反馈调整介入时机，实现隐私友好的端侧深度个性化主动助理。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13457"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出“深度个性化”闭环，使智能体持续观察用户活动、选择性保留长期上下文，并根据隐式反馈不断调整介入决策。Coco 将观察职责交给独立的 Observer Agent，利用用户参与、忽略、明确请求和重复工作模式等信号判断何时提供帮助。系统采用高效端侧策略运行，无需将原始桌面活动持续传输到云端。作者还从计算机操作轨迹合成 OSWorld-Persona 基准；适配后，三个模型骨干的宏平均 F1 从 0.12–0.19 提升至 0.35–0.48，一周用户研究中的 F1 则从 0.199 提升至 0.515。"
purpose = "- 为主动智能体提供“观察—记忆—反馈—适配”的个性化闭环，降低不合时宜的干预。\n- 展示如何利用参与、忽略和重复行为等隐式信号持续学习用户偏好，减少显式标注需求。\n- 端侧观察与紧凑上下文维护有助于兼顾实时性、计算效率和用户隐私。\n- OSWorld-Persona 的合成流程可用于在不收集敏感真实活动的情况下评估个性化介入策略。"
notes = "标题：Deep Personalization: Continuous Context Observation for Local Proactive Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13457\n摘要：As AI agents become increasingly capable, users must still recognize when and how to ask for help, leaving many opportunities for useful support unrealized. Proactive agents hold the promise to close this gap, but effective proactivity requires understanding users' real-time activities and personalizing intervention timing so that assistance is offered when it is likely to be helpful rather than disruptive. We introduce deep personalization, a closed-loop process that connects continuous context observation with continual agent adaptation: an agent observes user activity, selectively retains long-term context, and adapts intervention decisions from implicit feedback. We initiate deep personalization in Coco, a proactive desktop agent, by having a separate Observer Agent that monitors deskt"

content = "SLIC面向需要反复权衡耦合性能指标的模拟集成电路设计，将结构化电路表示映射为离散化的相对参数更新动作。框架采用领域与成功条件门控的多目标奖励，并提出奖励引导轨迹控制（RGRS），把逐步模拟器反馈同时用于策略学习和在线轨迹构造。作者发布了涵盖41种放大器拓扑的目标条件基准，支持分布内验证、未见拓扑评估和跨工艺节点迁移。基于开源65 nm CMOS PTM训练的4B模型在八轮预算下取得87.1%的分布内四指标Pass@1，并可迁移至五个未见工艺节点及商业TSMC 65 nm PDK；增加测试时模拟器交互后，未见拓扑Pass@1最高达到83.2%。"
purpose = "- 为无法向外部服务传输专有PDK数据的芯片设计场景提供本地闭环优化方案。\n- 说明小语言模型结合领域动作空间、模拟器反馈和强化微调后，可在专用工程任务上显著超越超大通用模型。\n- RGRS展示了如何在稀疏奖励和强约束环境中，将在线反馈同时用于策略更新与候选轨迹构造。\n- 跨拓扑、跨工艺节点及商业PDK迁移结果表明，开放工艺数据训练的策略具有实际部署潜力。"
notes = "标题：SLIC: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning Small LMs for Multi-Turn Analog Circuit Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13452\n摘要：Analog integrated circuit (IC) design is a multi-turn, simulator-grounded engineering task that demands iterative reasoning over coupled performance tradeoffs, yet proprietary process design kits (PDKs) make hosted large language model workflows difficult to deploy in practical design settings. We present SLIC (Small LM Iterating on Circuits), a reinforcement fine-tuning framework that trains a 4B-scale, locally deployable language model for closed-loop analog circuit optimization. SLIC pairs structured circuit representations with discretized relative-update actions, domain- and success-gated multiobjective rewards, and Reward-Guided Rollout Steering (RGRS), which turns per-step simulator feedback into signals for both policy learning and online rollout construction in this sparse, highly"

notes = "标题：BioMem: Agentic Memory for Biomedical LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13426\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable potential in biomedical applications, while agentic memory systems have emerged as a promising approach to preserving and utilizing information across tasks and interactions. In this study, we systematically investigate agentic memory in the biomedical domain, focusing on task effectiveness, retrieval quality, information fidelity, and long-term robustness. We evaluate five memory approaches across four LLMs in three representative settings: biomedical knowledge reasoning, clinical prediction, and long-term conversational memory. Our analyses reveal how memory construction, evidence r"

summary = "Looped CED 通过在编码器中实施随机深度递归与跨层、跨轮次 KV 共享，在不增加参数及轮次 KV 缓存的情况下获得更深计算，并显著提升长上下文服务容量与吞吐量。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13378"
code = ""

content = "论文提出循环因果编码器—解码器（Looped CED），仅由稀疏编码器层生成全局 KV，并将递归计算放在编码器侧以持续细化全局表示。随机递归深度训练促使编码器循环形成固定点平衡，使不同轮次能够无损共享最后一轮的 KV 缓存，实现轮次维度上的零缓存增长。结合跨层与跨轮次 KV 共享，该架构保持未循环 CED 的最低内存占用，同时恢复循环 Transformer 约 86%～93% 的性能增益，所需全局 KV 仅为后者的六分之一。在单个 4×GB200 节点上将 552B 参数的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 循环 8 轮时，其每词元 KV 占用降低 36.8 倍，可容纳 38 个 128K 词元请求，并在 8K 提示下实现 3.6 倍吞吐量。"
purpose = "- 为递归深度架构提供不随循环轮数增长的 KV 缓存设计，缓解推理服务中的显存瓶颈。\n- 表明非对称编码器—解码器应在编码器侧循环，以迭代更新全局 KV，而非重复读取静态解码器投影。\n- 可用于扩大长上下文请求的并发批量，并在固定硬件资源下提高线上推理吞吐量。\n- 固定点式 KV 共享为循环计算、缓存压缩及超大参数模型部署提供了可扩展路径。"
notes = "标题：Looping Deeper Without Growing the KV Cache: Recurrent Depth for Causal Encoder-Decoders\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13378\n摘要：Looped language models deepen computation without adding parameters, yet caching full-context key-value (KV) states across layers and rounds severely bounds serving batch size. We design a looped causal encoder-decoder (CED) that achieves recurrent depth with zero round-wise KV growth, addressing where and how to loop an asymmetric stack in which only sparse encoder layers generate global KV. We find that recurrence must be placed in the encoder to iteratively refine global KV representations rather than re-reading static decoder projections, which both drives language-modeling gains and preserves long-context scaling. Furthermore, training the encoder loop under stochastic recurrence depth induces a fixed-point equilibrium where every round losslessly shares the last round's KV cache. Com"

notes = "标题：Veritas: A Verifier Generation Harness for Agent Tasks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13336\n摘要：The effectiveness of a self-improving system relies heavily on the quality of its verifier, which judges the outcome of each improvement round. Unlike general-purpose agent harnesses, Veritas adds three mechanisms for verifier generation: an evidence header stating what the verifier will observe, a library of domain rules that tasks leave unstated, and authoring principles, partly refined by a self-improvement loop. content = "研究将前沿 LLM 智能体通过 Model Context Protocol（MCP）接入 GPU 评估器，并结合仅训练集拟合、持久记忆、自洽计算和外部数据集评估来搜索交换-相关泛函。Opus-5.5 智能体以约少 40 倍的 token 通过筛选，并找到验证与留出误差均低于 Codex（GPT-6-Astra）候选的结构。最终得到的 13 参数泛函 O159 使用平滑密度窗口调节同自旋相关项，相比重新拟合的无窗口对照，将 GSCDB137 重原子总能量误差降低 29%。自洽 O159 将 ωB97M-V 在 MGCDB84 留出测试集上的误差降低 11.7%，而每组约 400 次拟合的 SyFES 演化搜索未能超越 GAS22 种子。"
purpose = "- 展示 LLM 科研智能体可与高性能科学计算评估器闭环协作，自动提出并筛选具有明确物理意义的解析表达式。\n- 说明科学发现候选不能只依据训练或验证误差判断，还需经过自洽计算、留出测试和外部物理基准验证。\n- 为比较不同语言模型骨干的搜索效率提供案例，token 消耗与最终发现质量均可作为智能体评估指标。\n- 表明带有持久记忆和工具调用能力的智能体，在有限拟合预算下可能优于传统符号进化搜索。"

We connect frontier LLM agents to a GPU evaluator through the Model Context Protocol, with training-only fitting, persistent memory, and self-consistent and external evaluation. Opus-5.5 agents passed the screening gate with about 40-fold fewer tokens than Codex (GPT-6-Astra) agents and found a candidate with lower validation and held-out errors; only this candidate advanced to self-consi"

summary = "MRFoundry 将 MRI 专业知识、工具调用、项目级记忆与输出检查整合进智能体科研循环，并在 MRI Agent Bench 上显著提高任务完成率。"

content = "MRFoundry 是一个开源、可扩展的软件包，将 MRI 采集物理、数据规范和专业工具知识转化为智能体可执行的指导与输出检查。其项目本地记忆能够保存科研决策及相关证据，经过审核的贡献机制则支持研究者持续完善共享知识。研究者监督的案例覆盖序列审计和实采数据重建，并将工具调用、结果验证与决策记录连接为闭环工作流。配套的 MRI Agent Bench 覆盖 24 类任务和 10 个模型：使用 MRFoundry 后，Core 任务全检查通过率由 67.5% 提升至 78.3%，Hard 任务由 36.6% 提升至 44.8%。"

notes = "标题：MRFoundry: Knowledge-guided agent loops for end-to-end MRI research\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13289\n摘要：AI agents can write code, use scientific software, and revise computational workflows, opening new possibilities for MRI research. We present MRFoundry, an open-source, extensible package that connects MRI knowledge to agent actions and output checks. Project-local memory preserves decisions and evidence, while reviewed contributions allow researchers to improve shared guidance. Researcher-supervised examples demonstrate skill-guided sequence auditing and acquired-data reconstruction, connecting tool use, output checks, and documented decisions. Alongside MRFoundry, we introduce MRI Agent Bench to compare agents with and without the package across models and MRI tas"

content = "作者提出以轨迹为中心的 episode record，将候选故障与其起源、传播路径、检测信号、响应措施和最终后果关联起来，并标注其运行阶段、状态域及可覆盖的控制机制。研究完整审读 Who&When 和 MAD 语料中六个多智能体系统的 95 条失败轨迹，发现故障进入位置的中位数仅为任务进度的 0.18，主要源于错误决策或对任务状态的捏造。72% 的失败轨迹在最终失败前已包含反驳错误的证据，但其中 88% 没有智能体采取行动；31% 的轨迹虽检测到偏差，却没有响应机制清除异常状态。独立评判、任务准入时固定契约、依据环境重新核验信念可覆盖多数故障，其中 35 条轨迹仅恢复智能体自身状态即可挽救；此外，45% 的手工构造 Who&When 失败以内容过滤器崩溃告终，脚手架随后输出了并非任何智能体生成的答案。"

notes = "标题：Dependable Long-Horizon LLM Agents: A Trajectory-Centered Empirical Study of Where Errors Enter and Which Controls Reach Them\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13254\n摘要：When an LLM agent fails part-way through a long task, the usual analysis records a symptom: a failure-mode label, or the agent and step judged responsible. We define an episode record that ties a candidate fault to its origin, propagation, detection, response and consequence, located by operational phase and state domain, and that names the control families whose observation and reach cover it. We turned the record into a written annotation protocol and applied it to 95 failed runs of six multi-agent systems from the public Who&When and MAD corpora, reading each trajectory in full. content = "NCP 将预训练模型的推理数据平面与权重空间控制策略分离，根据运行遥测合成临时参数干预规则，并通过验证器决定是否接纳。系统结合 W-ISA 实现事务式执行与参数恢复，同时管理候选规则的优先级、生命周期、版本和评估档案。在 SmolLM-135M-Instruct 实验中，一个投影策略将验证集词元重复率从 0.4133 降至 0.2191，困惑度变化为 -0.0574%。由于三个候选策略均未带来新的精确答案，接纳门拒绝并归档全部候选，保留原始策略；独立进程复测和状态恢复实验复现了结果。"
purpose = "- 为控制策略层面的递归自我改进提供可执行、可审计的闭环框架。\n- 利用接纳门和独立验证避免将局部指标改善误判为整体能力提升。\n- 通过事务式权重干预与请求后参数恢复，降低在线自修改对基础模型造成持久损害的风险。\n- 版本化策略表与候选档案有助于复现实验、回滚策略并分析改进轨迹。"
notes = "标题：NCP: Verifier-Guided Optimization of Weight-Space Control Policies\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13236\n摘要：We present the Neural Control Plane (NCP), a closed-loop system that observes language-model execution, synthesizes candidate weight-space intervention rules, evaluates them, and maintains a versioned table of admitted policies. NCP separates a pretrained model's inference data plane from the control policy that selects temporary parameter interventions. W-ISA supplies transactional execution and parameter restoration; NCP supplies telemetry, candidate synthesis, admission criteria, priorities, and rule lifetime management. The optimization state consists of active rules and an archive of evaluated candidates, while base-model parameters are restored after each request. In a local SmolLM-135M-Instruct experiment, NCP generated three candidates from development telemetry and evaluated them "

summary = "研究表明，RLVR 中的参数跨提示共享会依据错误答案在提示间的接受覆盖度抑制或放大奖励作弊，而修复模型反复利用的验证器漏洞比单纯减少错误数量更有效。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13218"
code = ""

content = "研究在可精确计算完整响应分布的受控环境中，考察验证器静态假阳性率与错误答案跨提示通用性对 RLVR 奖励作弊的影响。在 KL 正则化最优解下，RLVR 仅重新加权验证器接受的响应集合而不改变其组成，因此作弊率会被原样继承，可作为分析训练漂移的零假设。基于奖励与经验切线核协方差的分解，作者发现参数共享使接受同一错误答案的提示提高其概率，而拒绝该答案的提示则通过基线效应压低其概率。因而，只有少数提示接受的漏洞会被抑制，多数提示接受的通用漏洞则会被放大；独立的逐提示策略副本可消除这种依赖。实验覆盖 Adam、SGD 与采样 GRPO，并显示针对模型跨提示重复利用的错误修补验证器时，即使移除同等数量的错误，真实准确率也可相差最多 38 个百分点。"
purpose = "- 为“RLVR 继承奖励作弊”与“RLVR 放大奖励作弊”两种看似冲突的观察提供统一解释。\n- 评估验证器漏洞时，应结合基础模型对错误答案的支持度及其跨提示共享范围，而不能只看静态假阳性率。\n- 验证器修复应优先覆盖模型会在多个提示中重复利用的系统性错误，而非仅按错误数量平均修补。\n- 参数共享结构、学习率与优化器会影响作弊率是否越过基础模型水平，应纳入 RLVR 安全审计。"
notes = "标题：Wrong the Same Way: Cross-Prompt Sharing Both Suppresses and Amplifies Reward Hacking in RLVR\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13218\n摘要：Two accounts of how reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) interacts with an imperfect verifier coexist: in one, reward hacking is inherited from the base model; in the other, RL amplifies it. We study both in a controlled environment small enough to compute the entire response distribution exactly. We vary the universality of a leak, or the fraction of prompts that accept the same wrong response, and the verifier's static false-positive rate. At its KL-regularized optimum, RLVR acts as a filter that reweights the accepted set without altering its composition, so the hack rate is inherited exactly. Taking that as a null model, a known decomposition writes the drift from it as a sum over prompts of covariances between reward and the empirical tangent kernel. The terms from ot"

In addition, we introduce Local Action Consistency (LAC), an offline metric that measures whether nearby observations in representation space are also associat"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13143"

content = "AIOA 让编码智能体围绕预设指标循环改进数值算法，并使用智能体无法修改的验证器保障结果正确性。该框架应用于原子模拟库 ASE，同时优化代码实现与算法本身：前者在结果不变的前提下提升运行速度，后者减少收敛所需的力评估次数。智能体使用 Python、NumPy 和 SciPy 将六个计算内核平均加速 22～24 倍。在未见问题上，最佳配置生成的优化器进行结构弛豫时所需力评估次数比 L-BFGS 少 3.1～3.4 倍，计算反应路径时比标准攀爬图像弹性带方法少 4.6～17 倍，并产生了两个未在既有文献中发现的算法组件。"
purpose = "- 展示编码智能体不仅能重写代码，还能自主发现更高效的数值优化算法。\n- 不可篡改验证器可作为自动程序进化的安全边界，避免智能体通过修改测试或降低精度来获取虚假性能提升。\n- 同时优化运行时间与力评估次数，为原子模拟及其他高成本科学计算降低计算开销提供了可复用范式。\n- 在未见问题上的性能提升表明，自动发现的优化器具备一定跨任务泛化能力。"
notes = "标题：Autonomous Optimization of Numerical Algorithms by an AI Agent: Application to Atomistic Simulation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13143\n摘要：Coding agents built on large language models, run in automated improvement loops, can discover more efficient algorithms. We present AIOA, a framework that brings this approach to the numerical algorithms of atomistic simulation: an agent improves an algorithm against a pre-specified metric in a loop guarded by verifiers it cannot alter. Applied to ASE, a widely used atomistic simulation library, AIOA targets two facets of an algorithm: its implementation, rewritten to run faster with unchanged results, and the method itself, redesigned to converge with fewer force evaluations. Agents made six kernels 22-24 times faster on average in Python with NumPy and SciPy. On unseen problems, optimizers from the best configuration needed 3.1-3.4 times fewer force evaluations than L-BFGS for structure"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13135"
code = ""

content = "研究关注多轮工具调用智能体中的在策略蒸馏：成功轨迹虽能提供事后指导，但不兼容的交互状态可能产生误导监督。作者将成功参考选择建模为自由目标最优传输问题，为每条失败轨迹分配最兼容的成功轨迹。由于潜在状态路径不可见，方法采用动作序列对齐作为兼容性的可观测代理，并以最长公共子序列（LCS）提取允许可恢复绕行的有序动作骨架。在 ALFWorld 与 τ³-bench 航空领域实验中，LCS 是唯一能在各项主要指标上稳定优于无参考 OPD 的选择器，并在零额外优化下迁移至零售领域。"
purpose = "- 为多轮智能体蒸馏提供基于轨迹兼容性而非随机或表面相似度的成功样本选择准则。\n- 可用于降低不兼容成功轨迹造成的错误事后监督，提高工具调用策略训练的稳定性。\n- LCS 无需访问隐式环境状态，适合应用于仅能观测动作与交互记录的实际智能体系统。\n- 跨领域迁移结果表明，动作序列对齐可能帮助蒸馏策略学习可复用的任务执行结构。"
notes = "标题：Which Successes Make Good References? Action-Sequence Alignment for Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13135\n摘要：On-policy distillation (OPD) reduces train-inference distribution mismatch by supervising a student on its own trajectories. For multi-turn tool-use agents, conditioning the teacher on a validated same-task successful trajectory as a reference can provide useful hindsight, but different successes may reach incompatible interaction states and offer misleading supervision. We cast successful-reference selection as a free-target optimal transport problem, which reduces to assigning each failed trajectory its most compatible successful reference. Because latent state paths are hidden, we use action-sequence alignment as an observable proxy for compatibility and instantiate it with longest common subsequence (LCS), which identifies a shared ordered backbone of actions while allowing recoverable"

score = 3

summary = "该研究用无需模型调用的确定性代码检查器实时验证推理步骤，并在原位反馈错误，使符号数学准确率提升 1.4～5.6 个百分点且仅增加 6%～7% 的词元开销。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13107"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究提出在线确定性验证机制，在 LLM 生成推理步骤的同时使用代码进行实时检查，无需额外调用验证器模型或占用 GPU。检查失败时，系统直接指出当前错误，让模型从出错位置继续推理，而非废弃完整轨迹并重新生成。该方法在符号数学中借助计算机代数系统以毫秒级速度核验显式表达式。在 ASyMoB 基准和三个开放权重模型上的实验显示，准确率提升 1.4～5.6 个百分点，错误答案净减少 1.8%～14.9%，词元用量仅增加 6%～7%。"
purpose = "- 将错误检测前移到推理生成过程中，避免错误前提持续传播并浪费推理预算。\n- 以确定性程序替代验证器 LLM，可降低 GPU 竞争和响应延迟。\n- 支持从错误发生处继续生成，为低成本、可组合的推理时纠错提供实现路径。\n- 可扩展至更多具备可执行检查器的数学、科学与工程推理任务。"
notes = "标题：Catch It Where It Happens: Real Time Error Correction During LLM Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13107\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted with mathematical, scientific and engineering work, yet their mistakes are often confidently written and hard to spot. A single wrong step tends to propagate, making the model waste inference tokens following a false premise. Existing safeguards either check only the final answer and restart from scratch, or rely on a verifier LLM that competes for GPU capacity and slows down response time. We propose online, deterministic verification: many kinds of reasoning steps can be validated by code, so a step can be verified in real time, as it is written, without any model call or GPU use. Whenever a check fails, we point out the mistake and let the model continue from the same spot. We demonstrate this approach on symbolic mathematics, where "

notes = "标题：HiVe-Mem: Interleaving Horizontal Memory Expansion and Vertical Evidence Composition for Multimodal Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12872\n摘要：As multimodal agents accumulate increasingly long interaction histories, effectively retrieving and utilizing past information becomes critical for long-term interaction. For horizontal memory expansion, \\hivemem organizes memory episodes into a relational graph using textual and visual anchors, expanding semantically retrieved seeds with related neighbors to recover complementary inf"

content = "WorkflowArena将工作流编排定义为智能体自主决定是否委派、如何拆分任务以及如何组织工作智能体的一项独立能力。基准包含横跨软件工程、形式数学、法律分析和三维制作等8个专业领域的145项长程任务，每项任务要求完成由相互依赖组件构成的集成交付物。隐藏验证器负责评估交付质量，系统同时记录全部委派行为，从而联合分析任务结果与底层工作组织方式。九种模型的实验显示，不同系统具有明显不同的委派策略，且没有单一配置能在所有领域占优；由Claude Opus 5.5编排的工作流还可显著改善较弱执行模型的表现。"
purpose = "- 为多智能体系统提供独立于端到端成功率的工作流编排能力评测框架。\n- 可用于比较不同模型在任务拆分、委派频率、工作组规模及跨智能体协作方面的策略差异。\n- 表明编排模型与执行模型可以解耦，利用更强模型生成工作流有望提升较弱执行器的长程任务表现。\n- 隐藏验证器与完整委派轨迹可支持对交付质量、组织结构和执行过程的联合审计。"
notes = "标题：WorkflowArena: Benchmarking Executable Workflow Orchestration in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12856\n摘要：Large language model agents are increasingly deployed as multi-agent systems rather than as a standalone model operating within a single context. Recent agent harnesses allow the model to instantiate worker agents and to author the executable workflow that coordinates them, shifting the design of system structure from developers to the model at run time. The quality of this organization therefore constitutes a distinct capability, yet existing benchmarks either measure end-to-end task completion or evaluate coordination under structures specified in advance. We formalize this capability as workflow orchestration and introduce WorkflowArena, a benchmark of 145 long-horizon tasks spanning 8 professional domains, from software engineering and formal mathematics to legal analysis and 3D produc"

summary = "研究通过向10篇心理学论文植入100个已知错误，发现最佳单一AI评审系统检出71项，而集成全部系统可检出93项，但所有系统均难以识别信息遗漏类错误。"

content = "研究者在10篇开放获取的心理学论文中植入100个已知错误，并使用前沿模型及两款商业AI评审工具进行检测。最佳单一系统检出71项错误，最差系统仅检出30项；汇总所有系统的结果后，检出率提升至93%。不同系统发现的错误仅部分相关，表明集成评审能够显著扩大问题覆盖范围，其中 Refine.ink 提供了最多的独有检出结果，但单位错误成本较高。未被任何系统发现的7项错误全部属于信息遗漏，同时实验未测量误报率，植入错误与真实论文错误分布的一致性也尚不明确。"

notes = "标题：How Well Does AI Peer Review Work? A Benchmark Built from 100 Planted Errors\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12820\n摘要：One hundred known errors were planted into 10 open-access psychology papers, and the modified papers were then run through frontier models and two commercial AI review tools. The best single system caught 71 of the 100 errors, while the worst caught 30. Pooling every system's output caught 93. Models are only partly correlated in the errors they find, which makes ensembling a big lever for finding issues in papers. Seven errors could not be caught by any system, and all seven were omissions: information deleted from a paper rather than mistakes inserted into it. Refine.ink contributes more unique catches than any other single system, though it is expensive on a per-error basis. False positives were not measured, and how this error distribution compares to the distribution of errors in real"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Agentic-OPD将强化学习过程中保存的历史检查点作为免费教师池，通过动态教师选择和局部监督过滤恢复智能体已遗忘的任务能力，在24个基准及任务子集上最高提升7.4分。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12799"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究发现，GRPO训练中的最佳验证检查点会遗忘早期检查点已经解决的问题，而所有检查点的能力并集显著超过任一单独检查点。Agentic-OPD以最佳验证检查点为学生、全部历史检查点为冻结教师候选，将一次已完成的强化学习训练转化为可复用的策略池。HCN（Highest Coverage Next）策略根据学生尚未解决的问题动态选择覆盖率最高的互补教师，LAD（Locality-Aware Distillation）则仅在教师优于学生的局部失败轨迹上执行蒸馏，避免覆盖学生已有能力。该方法在5类智能体任务、24个基准及任务子集上带来最高7.4分提升，其中4B学生模型在深度搜索任务上超过文中对比的前沿模型。"
purpose = "- 说明仅保留最佳验证检查点会造成能力覆盖损失，历史检查点应被视为具有互补能力的策略资产。\n- 可在不增加前期训练成本的情况下，从已有强化学习运行中构建多教师池并恢复遗忘能力。\n- 动态教师选择与局部蒸馏过滤可减少错误教师监督和能力覆盖冲突。\n- 为强化学习后的能力恢复提供独立于RL算法改进的后处理阶段。"
notes = "标题：Agentic-OPD: Recovering Forgotten Agent Capabilities via On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Tasks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12799\n摘要：Reinforcement learning is now the mainstream paradigm for training LLMs on agentic tasks, yet standard practice keeps only the best-validation checkpoint and discards the rest. We show that this is lossy: in GRPO training of LLM agents, the best-validation checkpoint fails on problems that earlier checkpoints from the same run had already solved, and the union of problems solved across checkpoints far exceeds any single one. The discarded checkpoints thus form a teacher pool obtained at no additional training cost, which on-policy distillation (OPD) can mine to recover the lost coverage. Mining this pool raises two challenges: i) which checkpoint to distill from, as no checkpoint dominates the others and complementarity to the student is problem-specific; ii) where to trust it, as even a w"


[[items]]
id = "agentopsd"
title = "AgentOPSD：递归信念修正实现轮次级信用分配"
subtopic = "信用分配"
topics = [
    "信用分配",
    "强化学习",
    "智能体",
    "长程任务",

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "AgentOPSD通过递归贝叶斯信念修正将轨迹级优势重新分配到关键交互轮次，在无需评论器的情况下提升长程智能体强化学习效果。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12587"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "AgentOPSD针对可验证奖励强化学习中轨迹级优势粒度过粗的问题，提出逐交互轮次进行信用分配的无评论器方案。方法汇总技能条件自教师与学生之间的词元级对数概率差，并将其作为证据，在对数几率空间中递归执行贝叶斯更新。更新结果通过有界且保持符号的乘数，将结果优势重新分配至不同轮次。以Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-7B为策略骨干在ALFWorld上测试时，该方法稳定优于GRPO及自蒸馏基线，7B模型成功率达到89.1%。消融实验表明，轮次边界上的证据聚合与依赖历史的信念修正是性能提升的关键。"
purpose = "- 为长程、多轮智能体任务提供比整条轨迹共享优势更精细的轮次级信用分配机制。\n- 无需额外训练评论器，可降低价值估计带来的系统复杂度与误差。\n- 展示了将自蒸馏差异转化为递归贝叶斯证据的路径，可用于改进RLVR和智能体后训练。\n- 提示信用分配应同时利用轮次边界与历史信息，而非仅依赖当前局部教师—学生差异。"
notes = "标题：AgentOPSD: Turn-Level Credit Assignment via Recursive Belief Revision\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12587\n摘要：Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards ordinarily spreads a single advantage over a whole trajectory, a granularity too coarse to isolate the few decisions on which success in a long, multi-turn interaction actually turns. Privileged self-distillation supplies denser supervision, but it leaves unanswered how a set of local teacher-student discrepancies should be converted into credit along a sequence. We introduce AgentOPSD, a critic-free scheme that assigns credit recursively, one interaction turn at a time. Inside a turn, the token-level log-probability gaps between a skill-conditioned self-teacher and the student are summed, and that sum acts as evidence in a recursive Bayesian update carried out in log-odds space; the resulting revisions reallocate the outcome-derived advantage"

content = "SciMARS面向闭环科学发现，对多智能体脚手架进行任务局部的递归适应，以解决证据遗失、重复工作和固定协作流程持续放大错误的问题。框架使用共享证据图保存不断演化的科学证据，并以独立组件编辑器调整智能体角色与协调方式；SciMARS + Memory进一步编辑证据的记录、检索和分发策略。候选探针用于指导下一版脚手架的组合与选择，评测覆盖实验数据回放、数字孪生及交互式任务。在Autonomous Science Bench、ScienceAgentBench和AppWorld上，加入记忆编辑的版本整体优于基础多智能体团队，例如在AppWorld上的任务完成数由5/20提升至11/20。"
purpose = "- 为闭环科研智能体提供“共享证据记忆＋脚手架递归适应”的系统设计范式。\n- 表明仅保留历史证据并不足够，还需持续优化角色分工、交接流程以及记忆读写策略。\n- 可用于构建能够根据实验反馈自我调整的科研工作流，并减少重复分析和错误传播。\n- 共享证据图与独立组件编辑器的解耦设计也可迁移到长程交互和复杂工具调用任务。"
notes = "标题：SciMARS: Multi-Agent Recursive Self-Improvement for Closed-Loop Scientific Discovery\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12552\n摘要：We propose SciMARS, a framework for recursive task-local adaptation of multi-agent harnesses in closed-loop scientific discovery. Multi-agent systems coordinate analysis, experimental design, and verification as scientific evidence evolves. However, two problems limit their effectiveness: (i) without shared memory, agents lose observations and repeat work; (ii) fixed role procedures, handoffs, and memory policies perpetuate mistakes despite retained evidence. We address these problems with a shared evidence graph and separate component editors. SciMARS adapts roles and coordination; SciMARS + Memory adds a third editor for how agents record, retrieve, and receive evidence. Candidate probes guide composition and selection of the next harness. We evaluate scientific discovery through experim"

content = "研究让四个智能体分别完成简短算术任务，并将同一键值存储伪装成各自独立的草稿空间，其中两个智能体持有互补的计算片段。实验未在提示中提及其他智能体、共享状态或协作，但联合成功仍可通过共享存储出现。协调与模型供应商层级无明显关系，关键在于智能体是否写入中间结果并再次检查存储；一句“提交前再次检查工作区”即可开关这种行为。研究按照书面协议独立重建第二套实现，并通过设计、数据和分析的哈希锁定完成复现，结果支持将共享状态视为需要隔离和审计的通信通道。"
purpose = "- 提醒多智能体部署方对缓存、记忆和工作区实施命名空间隔离，避免意外的信息交换与协同行为。\n- 将共享状态的读写记录纳入轨迹审计，以识别未显式授权的跨智能体通信。\n- 说明细微的提示变化可能决定智能体是否重新读取共享状态，应纳入安全评测和回归测试。\n- 为多智能体实验提供预注册式锁定设计与独立实现复现范式，降低分析自由度带来的偏差。"
notes = "标题：Unprompted coordination through shared memory in LLM agents: a locked two-implementation replication and extension\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12412\n摘要：Coordination between large language-model agents has become a central question for AI safety and software engineering, as such agents are deployed in groups that share infrastructure. Existing research tells the agents that partners exist or asks them to coordinate. Whether coordination arises through shared state alone, with nothing disclosing that anyone else is present, is the key question for deployments that share caches and memory between agents, yet it has rarely been examined, and never with independent replication; this study does both. Four agents each received a short arithmetic task and a key-value store presented to each as its own scratch space; two held complementary halves of one computation, and no prompt mentioned other agents, sharing or collaboration. For reliability, t"


[[items]]
id = "item-106"
title = "示例解答会掩盖自生成训练数据中的规则组合能力成本"
subtopic = "数据合成"
topics = [
    "数据合成",
    "模型训练",
    "组合泛化",

]

]

    "数据合成",

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "在2B推理模型学习新造定量规则的实验中，加入程序生成的示例解答虽可降低训练成本并保持单步准确率，却显著削弱规则的组合复用能力并增加成功推理的生成成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12225"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究比较两种自生成训练目标：仅提供规则表，以及同时提供规则表和程序编写的示例解答；生成目标通过最终答案正确性筛选，随后移除辅助材料进行微调和评估。训练六轮后，仅规则表方案在前瞻性组合测试中领先24.3个百分点；面对未见参数值时，两组单步准确率同为87.5%，但组合准确率分别为58.3%和23.6%。独立采样的第二组目标库复现了相同趋势，而增加较短示例解答目标的训练轮数只能缩小、不能稳定消除差距。尽管示例解答减少了采集词元和更新时间，其训练出的模型每次组合成功所需生成词元约为仅规则表方案的2.6倍，说明单步准确率不足以衡量新规则的可复用性。"
purpose = "- 设计自生成训练数据时，应同时评估规则的单步应用与多步组合复用，避免被练习任务准确率误导。\n- 示例解答能够降低数据生成和更新成本，但可能诱导捷径学习，削弱对规则结构的内化。\n- 数据策略比较应纳入每次成功推理的生成词元、训练时间和组合泛化能力，而非只比较训练阶段成本。\n- 可使用程序校验的数值签名和独立目标库复现实验，提高规则学习评估的可靠性。"
notes = "标题：A Cost That Practice Accuracy Hides: Worked Solutions in Self-Generated Training Data and the Compositional Use of New Rules\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12225\n摘要：A worked solution can make self-generated training targets shorter and cheaper, but accuracy on the practiced operation may hide how well a model can reuse newly learned rules. We study this choice in a 2B-parameter reasoning model learning two invented quantitative rules. The model generates targets from a rule sheet alone or from the sheet plus a program-written worked solution; targets are accepted for final-answer correctness, and the support is removed for fine-tuning and evaluation. Training questions request forward use in one segment; composition requires sequential reuse of the same rule across two segments. After six passes, using a program-checked numerical signature, sheet-only targets lead by 24.3 percentage points (95% item-bootstrap CI 14.6-34.0) on composition in a prospect"


[[items]]
id = "bf16"
title = "bf16 循环状态舍入导致混合推理模型过度思考"
subtopic = "推理效率"
topics = [
    "推理",
    "人工智能可靠性",
    "成本优化",
    "系统优化",
    "模型评估",
]
research = [

]
keywords = [
    "bf16",
    "随机舍入",
    "循环状态",
    "推理效率",
    "Gated DeltaNet",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现，使用 bf16 最近舍入缓存循环状态会使多数混合推理模型生成更长、陷入重复循环并降低准确率，而同位宽随机舍入可基本消除该问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12127"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在 6 个开源混合推理模型和 590 道竞赛数学题上评估 bf16 循环状态缓存，发现采用最近舍入（RTN）时，5 个模型的几何平均输出长度增加至 1.07～1.27 倍，触及 32K token 上限的次数也更多。Qwen3.5-9B 的截断样本数由 118 增至 223，准确率从 85.6% 降至 70.8%；在 LiveCodeBench 上，两个受测模型的 pass@1 分别下降 12.6 和 13.4 个百分点。将预算提高至 128K 仍无法完全弥合差距，且 85% 的未完成输出属于精确重复循环，表明低精度状态会改变模型的推理动力学。采用同位宽 bf16 随机舍入（SR）后，多数受影响模型的长度膨胀消失，Qwen3.5-9B 的准确率恢复至 87.6%，说明主要根因是舍入偏差而非 bf16 分辨率不足。"
purpose = "- 混合推理模型评测应同时报告循环状态数据类型、输出长度和 token 上限触发率，避免仅用准确率掩盖数值格式造成的行为偏移。\n- 部署 Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet 等循环混合架构时，可用 bf16 随机舍入替代最近舍入，在基本保持显存与解码速度的同时改善准确率和推理成本。\n- 重复循环和异常长输出可作为循环状态数值误差的诊断信号，并用于服务引擎的回归测试。\n- 部分模型只需对特定衰减区间的注意头启用随机舍入，提示可进一步探索按头选择的低开销精度策略。"
notes = "标题：Lost in Rounding: bf16 Recurrent State Makes Hybrid Reasoning Models Think Longer\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12127\n摘要：Hybrid reasoning models interleave attention with recurrent mixers such as Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet, whose state is cached between decoding steps. Prior work showed that storing this state in FP16 or INT8 with round-to-nearest (RTN) makes Mamba-2 and GDN hybrids more verbose, and that stochastic rounding (SR) fixes it. We study the other 16-bit format that serving engines expose, bf16, on six open hybrid reasoning models and 590 competition-math problems. With bf16 RTN state, five of six models reason longer (geometric-mean length 1.07x to 1.27x; also under greedy decoding in the two models tested) and hit the 32K token cap more often. On Qwen3.5-9B, cap hits rise from 118 to 223 and accuracy falls from 85.6% to 70.8%. On LiveCodeBench, both models tested also reason longer and lose 12.6"

summary = "论文提出智能体脚手架中间层平台 Agency，在不修改模型或降低任务准确率的情况下，通过减少词元消耗与 LLM 调用将现有脚手架的执行成本最高降低 35%。"

code = ""

content = "论文分析多种智能体执行轨迹中的成本累积模式，指出脚手架配置对准确率影响有限，却可能使同一模型与任务的成本相差最高 2.6 倍。研究从系统层面识别减少词元使用和 LLM 调用的优化机会，无需修改底层模型。作者据此设计统一的脚手架中间层平台 Agency，可将优化策略便携地应用于 Claude Code、OpenAI Codex 等现有系统。概念验证实验表明，Agency 在多项真实世界基准上最高可节省 35% 的成本，同时保持任务准确率。"
purpose = "- 表明智能体降本除模型压缩外，还可从脚手架与执行轨迹入手进行系统级优化。\n- Agency 的中间层抽象有助于跨不同智能体框架复用词元压缩、调用削减等优化策略。\n- 为生产环境建立准确率不变约束下的成本分析、调用审计与资源治理机制提供参考。"
notes = "标题：System Optimizations for Cost-Efficient Agent Harnesses\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12122\n摘要：AI agent harnesses play the critical role of providing LLMs with an interface to a computing environment. Recent studies show that AI agents' task-execution accuracy depends mostly on the LLM used, while harness-side variances such as system prompts or tools have minimal impact. Rather, such harness-side variances show a larger impact on the cost of agent executions, achieving cost reductions up to 2.6 times on the same model and task. To further explore these harness-side cost optimization opportunities, we analyze the cost accumulation patterns across various agent traces and identify system opportunities to reduce token use and LLM calls without modifying the model or affecting task accuracy. Based on this analysis, we design Agency, a harness interposition platform that provides a unif"


[[items]]
id = "scale-llm"
title = "SCALE：小模型优先的概念抽象与 LLM 引导经验内化"
subtopic = "经验学习"

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "SCALE 通过小模型常规决策、LLM 选择性反思和分层概念内化，将跨回合经验转化为轻量模型可独立复用的抽象知识。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12120"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SCALE 采用小模型优先的运行方式，由轻量模型处理常规预测，仅在出现预测错误或积累足够跨回合证据时触发 LLM 反思。LLM 检索相关历史经验，生成正向与负向反事实样例，并提出可复用的概念假设。经独立验证的概念被逐步写入分层神经表征，已经掌握的经验则移出活跃记忆，同时在完整档案中保留原始记录。实验显示，历史回忆将留出集准确率从 87.11% 提升至 98.28%，分层概念知识将困难样例准确率从 55.33% 提升至 66.47%，抽象归档还把活跃经验记录由 6,000 条压缩至 1,528 条。"
purpose = "- 为持续学习智能体提供一种低成本架构，使昂贵的 LLM 推理仅用于高价值错误分析与知识抽象。\n- 展示如何将离散经验和反思结果内化为小模型可直接调用的分层概念，而非长期依赖外部提示或完整轨迹。\n- 通过“活跃记忆加完整档案”的分层管理方式，在保留可追溯经验的同时降低在线检索与存储负担。\n- 正负反事实样例与独立概念验证可用于减少错误概念固化，提升知识复用和跨样例泛化能力。"
notes = "标题：SCALE: Small-Model-First Concept Abstraction with LLM-Guided Experience Internalization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12120\n摘要：Autonomous agents must continually acquire reusable knowledge from experience without relying on costly large language model (LLM) reasoning for every decision. However, existing experience-based approaches often struggle to transform accumulated observations and reasoning into compact knowledge that lightweight models can independently apply. This paper proposes SCALE, a small-model-first framework that integrates selective LLM reasoning, experience-driven concept abstraction, and knowledge internalization. A lightweight model handles routine predictions, while prediction errors and accumulated cross-episode evidence selectively trigger LLM reflection. The LLM analyzes relevant historical experiences, generates positive and negative counterfactual examples, and proposes reusable concept h"

id = "skillduet"
title = "SkillDuet：基于响应反馈的模型与技能协同演化"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "SkillDuet利用模型响应反馈协调参数更新与文本技能演化，实现技能筛选、持久化及技能级递归自我改进。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12109"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SkillDuet将强化学习经验同时内化到模型参数，并外化为可复用的文本技能，使二者在训练过程中协同演化。框架从在策略轨迹中提取技能，并通过 OPD 持续引导参数更新；其响应评分指标 Strength 根据技能对当前模型回答造成的变化幅度对候选技能排序。高分技能会被定期整合至持久化的全局技能集 Global，再与更新后的模型共同指导后续交互。基于 Qwen3-4B 的实验在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上分别取得 98.57% 与 92.97% 的成功率，较匹配的 SEED-API 基线提升 2.86 和 5.47 个百分点。消融实验表明，响应驱动的技能选择与全局技能整合均能提升性能，但技能收益取决于其内容及其与当前模型的兼容性。"
purpose = "- 为参数学习与外部技能库的联合更新提供具体机制，避免静态技能随模型能力变化而失效。\n- 可将模型响应变化幅度作为技能价值与兼容性的动态筛选信号。\n- 展示了通过在策略经验、技能持久化和参数更新构建技能级递归自我改进闭环的可行性。\n- 适用于需要持续积累、筛选和复用任务经验的交互式智能体训练。"
notes = "标题：SkillDuet: Co-Evolving Models and Skills through Response Feedback\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12109\n摘要：During reinforcement learning, experience can be both internalized in LLM parameters and retained externally as reusable textual skills. Parameter updates alter the model's behavior and the interaction experience from which new skills are extracted. These skills, in turn, guide the model's subsequent interactions and parameter updates. As the model's capabilities evolve, previously beneficial skills may become redundant or detrimental. We present SkillDuet, a framework that coordinates parameter updates and skill evolution through model response feedback. Skills extracted from on-policy trajectories guide parameter updates through OPD at every step. A response-based score, Strength, ranks candidate skills by the magnitude of the shift they induce in the current model's responses, and the h"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12068"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "MIRIX-2.0由六类记忆智能体保存完整记录，并以带来源引用的溯源超图索引情景记忆和语义记忆。系统在显式预算下执行检索，使单次问题检查的证据规模不随历史记录持续增长。AutoDream在写入路径之外合并重复锚点，不改写原始记录，同时将任务会话蒸馏为可复用技能。该系统在LoCoMo上达到91.4%、在LongMemEval-S上达到72.6%，并在ALFWorld、WebShop和MetaClaw-Bench等程序性记忆任务中表现领先。MIRIX-2.0无需微调，兼容闭源与开放权重模型；关闭AutoDream后，其在五个计时基准上均快于HyperMem。"
purpose = "- 为长期运行智能体提供兼顾完整记录、检索准确性与计算效率的非参数记忆架构。\n- 溯源超图可保留答案证据与原始记录之间的引用关系，有助于记忆审计和结果追溯。\n- 预算化检索为控制历史增长带来的延迟与成本提供了可复用的系统设计。\n- 将任务会话蒸馏为技能，展示了长期记忆支持经验复用和程序性能力积累的路径。"
notes = "标题：MIRIX-2.0: Multi-Agent Memory System\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12068\n摘要：Long-term memory lets LLM agents personalize, stay consistent, and reuse what worked before. Parametric memories need training, consolidation can lose details for good, and keeping every record leaves a growing store to search. We present MIRIX-2.0, a multi-agent memory system designed to be practical, accurate, and efficient at once. It is the most accurate of the compared systems on four of five QA benchmarks. It reaches 91.4% on LoCoMo and 72.6% on LongMemEval-S, where it leads the strongest baseline by 14.3 points, and with AutoDream disabled it gains 31.0 and 22.0 points over HyperMem on single-hop and multi-hop document QA. As procedural memory, it yields the highest mean success among the compared memory methods on ALFWorld and WebShop and reaches 49.2% on MetaClaw-Bench, against 44"

content = "SMO 将技能的核心抽象为 State-Move-Outcome 程序转移，而非依赖自然语言表述，并将其组织成外部符号图。编译新技能时，系统优先检索和组合已有砖块，通过 Outcome-to-State 条件动态连接，仅为尚未覆盖的过程创建候选砖块，并在验证后纳入复用库。在 SkillsBench v1.1 上，SMO 用 95 个共享砖块表示来自 87 个任务的 232 个原始技能，将表示规模从 366,472 tokens 降至 149,154 tokens，实现 59.3% 缩减和 2.46 倍压缩。其平均任务得分由 0.6379 提升至 0.7478，在 208/232 个实例上不弱于原始技能，并将平均运行时 token 消耗降低 38.38%。"
purpose = "- 将不断增长的自然语言技能库转化为可比较、去重和组合的结构化程序单元。\n- 为智能体技能库提供“检索—组合—验证—准入”的增量维护机制，减少重复技能积累。\n- 通过共享程序砖块同时优化技能表示成本、推理 token 消耗与下游执行效果。\n- 可用于构建具备技能复用、技能压缩和持续扩展能力的智能体执行框架。"
notes = "标题：SMO: Compiling Natural Language Skills into Graphs of Reusable State-Move-Outcome Bricks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12041\n摘要：Large language model agents increasingly rely on reusable natural-language skills, yet growing skill collections remain accumulations of isolated text artifacts that are difficult to compare, deduplicate, and compose. We argue that the reusable content of a skill lies not in its surface wording but in its procedural transitions: which operation to execute under a given state and which observable outcome to produce. Based on this observation, we propose State-Move-Outcome (SMO), a compilation-like framework that decomposes raw skills into reusable procedural bricks and organizes admitted bricks as an externalized symbolic structure. When compiling a skill, SMO first retrieves and composes existing bricks, dynamically connects them through Outcome-to-State conditions, and creates a candidate"

summary = "受控实验发现，生成式 AI 并未显著改善初学者的代码理解成绩，却使客观认知负荷增加 11.2%，且参与者未能准确感知这一额外负担。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12019"

content = "研究采用 3×2 被试内设计，让 20 名计算机专业初学者在有或无 AI 辅助的条件下阅读不同长度的 Java 代码，并完成无 AI 辅助的理解测验。tDRT 连续测量显示，使用 AI 时反应时间增加 11.2%，表明双任务交互带来了额外的客观认知负荷，但 NASA-TLX 与 SEQ 等主观评分未呈现相应变化。两种条件下的代码理解成绩均较高，AI 辅助未显示显著收益，且测验的天花板效应限制了对复杂任务收益的判断。参与者倾向于核验复杂代码的 AI 解释，却会直接相信简单代码的输出；部分人还从阅读源码转向略读或以 AI 解释替代源码。"
purpose = "- 提醒 AI 编程工具设计者关注工具交互产生的双任务成本，而不能只依据用户的主观轻松感评估体验。\n- 代码理解工具应减少上下文切换，并支持解释与源码的快速对照和验证。\n- 编程教育中应培养学习者核验 AI 输出、保持源码阅读能力，避免过度依赖生成式解释。\n- 后续评测应采用难度更高的真实代码任务，并结合实时客观指标与主观量表衡量认知负荷。"
notes = "标题：Feeling Easier, Working Harder: Cognitive Load in AI-Assisted Code Comprehension\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12019\n摘要：Software developers spend significant time reading and understanding code, increasingly supplemented by generative AI. While these tools aim to reduce the difficulty of reading code, interacting with them introduces a dual-task environment that may impose extraneous cognitive load that is challenging to capture. This study investigates how access to generative AI affects developers' real-time cognitive workload, code comprehension performance, and self-perceived code comprehension strategies. We conducted a 3x2 within-subjects study with 20 novice CS students reviewing Java code snippets of varying lengths with or without AI assistance, followed by an AI-unassisted comprehension quiz. Cognitive workload was continuously captured using a Detection-Response Task (tDRT) during both reading an"


[[items]]

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "推测式自蒸馏通过让教师仅在师生词元分布显著分歧时介入，在减少38%至69%监督词元的同时兼顾知识内化准确率、泛化能力与抗遗忘表现。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12016"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出推测式自蒸馏（SSD），用于将预训练之外的新事实、持续更新的知识或领域技能内化到语言模型中。学生默认负责生成训练轨迹，教师仅在双方下一词元分布显著分歧的位置介入，从而形成逐词元混合策略。该设计使训练轨迹贴近学生测试时的行为，同时避免完全在策略采样进入低信号状态。在三个闭卷问答基准上，SSD以减少38%至69%监督词元的代价接近在策略蒸馏效果，并持续优于离策略蒸馏；遗忘压力测试中也取得最佳域内准确率和更好的通用能力保持。"
purpose = "- 为闭卷知识内化提供监督成本更低的自蒸馏方案，适合频繁更新事实或学习新领域技能。\n- 通过师生分布分歧触发教师干预，可将监督预算集中于特权上下文真正改变预测的位置。\n- 在准确率、训练效率与灾难性遗忘之间建立更优的帕累托前沿，可用于持续学习和模型知识更新。\n- 逐词元混合策略为协调在策略与离策略蒸馏的优缺点提供了通用设计思路。"
notes = "标题：Speculative Self-Distillation enables Efficient Knowledge Internalization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12016\n摘要：Many real-world applications require language models to internalize knowledge that was absent from pretraining, to incorporate continually updated facts, or to learn new domain skills. Self-distillation has emerged as a natural approach for this task. A model conditioned on the source material acts as the teacher, and an unconditioned student is trained to reproduce the teacher's behavior closed-book. We propose Speculative Self-Distillation (SSD), an efficient per-token mixed-policy method for self-distillation. The student generates each rollout by default, while the teacher intervenes only at positions where its next-token distribution substantially diverges from the student's. Unlike off-policy distillation with teacher-generated prefixes, this design keeps training rollouts close to t"

summary = "Infrathon 以24小时长程任务评测语言智能体优化真实 AI 基础设施的能力，揭示评测时长会显著影响优化收益与模型排名。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11995"
code = ""

content = "Infrathon 收录了跨不同层级的真实世界 AI 基础设施优化任务，用于评估前沿语言智能体能否持续改进复杂系统。研究对6个模型开展150次、每次最长24小时的运行，总计算量超过1万 GPU 小时，其中135次运行在预算后半程仍取得进一步提升。模型排名会随评测时长变化，说明短时评测可能同时遗漏后期优化收益和模型相对性能变化。智能体还可发现超越人工基线的方案，例如 Claude Opus 5.5 将 KV-cache 平均压缩率从6.82%提升至19.86%，但任务违规、测试不可靠和过早结束仍会阻碍持续改进。"
purpose = "- 为构建和评估真实世界长程基础设施优化智能体提供统一基准与任务框架。\n- 提示智能体评测应采用更长时间预算，并报告性能随时间变化的轨迹，而非仅比较短时终点结果。\n- 可用于研究 KV-cache 压缩等系统优化任务中的自主搜索能力及其超越人工基线的潜力。\n- 任务违规、不可靠测试和过早结束可作为长程智能体运行时监控与执行验证的重点失效模式。"
notes = "标题：Infrathon: Benchmarking Language Agents for Real-World, Long-Horizon AI Infra Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11995\n摘要：In this work, we study whether frontier agent can continually improve real-world AI infrastructure over long horizons. We introduce Infrathon, a benchmark of diverse, long-horizon AI infrastructure optimization tasks across different levels. We evaluate six models across 150 runs with 24-hour budgets, totaling over 10,000 GPU-hours. Our results show that model rankings vary with the evaluation horizon, while 135 of the 150 runs achieve further improvements during the second half of their budgets. These findings indicate that shorter evaluations can overlook both optimization gains and changes in relative model performance. We also find agents can discover solutions that outperform human-designed baselines: Claude Opus 5.5 increases the mean KV-cache compression ratio from 6.82% with the hu"

id = "emoharness"
title = "EmoHarness：元脚手架演化增强多模态情绪理解"
subtopic = "脚手架演化"
topics = [
    "多模态",
    "智能体脚手架演化",

    "多模态推理",

    "模型评估",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "EmoHarness通过组织文本、韵律和视觉证据并离线演化调用脚手架，显著提升冻结小型多模态模型的情绪识别能力，但演化编辑本身的增益有限且未通过多重检验校正。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11994"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "EmoHarness将冻结的小型多模态学生模型置于由提示、调用设置和验证代码组成的脚手架中，并把视觉观察、转录文本及韵律描述编号为可引用证据。Meta-Harness Evolving（MHE）使用更强的Builder模型离线分析错误与执行轨迹、修改脚手架，再通过静态检查和验证门控筛选修改，且Builder无法接触评测样本。在5,507条MME-Emotion样本上，该系统相对单次纯视频调用将任务宏平均Recognition提高7.6至13.4个百分点，主要收益来自转录和韵律信息。演化修改最多额外提升0.95个百分点，且经Holm校正后均不显著；迁移至IntentQA的微小增益同样未通过校正。"
purpose = "- 展示无需更新学生模型权重，也可通过组织多模态证据和优化外围脚手架提升情绪理解能力。\n- 说明音频转录与韵律描述是主要增益来源，脚手架演化的独立贡献应通过消融实验和多重检验谨慎判断。\n- 提供一种受验证门控约束的离线脚手架演化范式，可用于低成本改进冻结模型及审计其执行轨迹。\n- 提醒后续工作关注多标签预测带来的指标变化，以及演化策略在跨任务迁移中的统计显著性。"
notes = "标题：EmoHarness: Boosting Multimodal Emotion Understanding with Meta-Harness Evolving\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11994\n摘要：Small multimodal models can misjudge emotion when words, voice, and visual cues conflict. We present EmoHarness, which runs such a model, the frozen student, inside a harness: the prompts, settings, and verification code around its calls. A human-written starting harness, the scaffold, numbers the student's visual observations, transcript, and prosody descriptions as citable evidence and has the student weigh candidate emotions against it. In Meta-Harness Evolving (MHE), a stronger LLM, the Builder, reads the student's errors and execution traces offline and edits the harness; static checks and validation gates decide which edits are kept, and the Builder never sees evaluation items. On 5,507 MME-Emotion items, EmoHarness raises task-macro Recognition by 7.6-13.4 points over a single video"

title = "FireGate：持续强化学习微调前的行为变化界定"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "FireGate通过在任务更新前设置基于冻结底层表征的提示级门控，以PAC保证在训练前界定持续强化学习微调造成的行为变化与遗忘风险。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11979"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "FireGate将每个任务的更新模块（如新LoRA）置于分类门控之后，由冻结的模型底层表征判断是否对当前提示启用更新。若没有门控触发，系统将精确执行旧模型的计算，从而把受保护提示分布上的行为变化上界限制为门控触发率。研究使用无需标注的受保护提示校准门控，并提供不依赖后续训练权重的PAC保证，将遗忘界定为门控的误报率。最多五个强化学习任务的连续训练实验中，门控方案仅改变3.3%的留出聊天响应，而无门控训练改变91.8%。该保证仅覆盖用于校准的提示分布，无法防御主动搜索可触发门控提示的对手。"
purpose = "- 在持续微调开始前给出可量化的行为变化上界，将遗忘风险控制从事后评测推进到事前认证。\n- 可用于保护既有技能、通用对话能力和拒答行为，降低连续部署新任务更新时的回归风险。\n- 揭示稳定性与新任务学习能力之间的权衡：更低的门控误报率会限制更新在新任务提示上的覆盖范围。\n- 实际部署时仍需关注校准分布偏移及对抗性提示，因为现有证书不覆盖主动搜索门控触发样本的攻击者。"
notes = "标题：FireGate: Bounding Behaviour Change Before Training in Continual RL Fine-Tuning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11979\n摘要：Fine-tuning a deployed language model on one task after another can change behaviour that should stay fixed, such as earlier skills, general chat or refusals. Today, such forgetting is found only after training, on the benchmarks someone chose to run. This work bounds, before training, how often its outputs change on chosen prompt distributions. FireGate places each task's update, such as a new LoRA, behind a gate, which is a classifier on the model's frozen lower layers that decides for each prompt whether the update is used. Where no gate fires, the model computes exactly what the old one computes. As a result, on any prompt distribution, every bounded behaviour score changes by at most the gates' firing rate there over a whole task stream. Calibrating each gate on unlabeled prompts of w"

The key challenge is that \\textit{actual quantization is expensive, while inexpensive proxies may not reflect quantized-model performance.} To address this challenge, we present AutoQS, an \\textbf{Auto}mated \\textbf{Q}uantization \\textbf{S}trategy Design with LLMs. AutoQS keeps weight precision fixed and searches group-size allocations across layers and operators under deployment constraints. Evaluation history guides coordinated LLM proposals, while deterministic validation ensures compatible allocations. An inexpen"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文提出 WebTrap Farm 评估框架，量化分析执行脚手架与后端模型对 Web 智能体安全韧性的共同影响，并定位不同执行阶段中的防御机制。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11971"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "论文将 Web 智能体脚手架抽象为目标解释、网页感知、推理规划和动作执行四阶段工作流。作者构建可扩展的 WebTrap Farm，覆盖恶意用户提示、恶意提示注入和欺骗性网页设计三类攻击。框架通过配对的可用性校准任务，将真实防御行为与智能体自身能力不足区分开来。实验发现现有脚手架普遍存在安全缺陷，部分不安全行为甚至会在没有显式攻击内容时出现；统计分析表明，脚手架设计和后端模型都会显著影响最终安全结果。"
purpose = "- 将执行脚手架视为 Web 智能体的重要安全边界，而非仅依赖后端模型的安全对齐。\n- 可利用四阶段工作流定位防御行为与失效环节，指导实施阶段化安全控制。\n- 通过可用性校准避免把任务失败误判为安全防御，提高安全评测的构念效度。\n- 为比较不同脚手架与后端模型组合提供系统化、可扩展的安全审计框架。"
notes = "标题：Harness Matters: Measuring and Dissecting Security Resilience in Web Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11971\n摘要：Web agents extend LLMs from text generation to real-world web interaction by relying on execution harnesses that interpret user goals, perceive webpages, plan actions, and ground model decisions into browser operations. While prior work has recognized that agent security depends on more than the backend model alone, how harness design quantitatively shapes web-agent security remains insufficiently measured. To systematically study web-agent harness security, we first characterize web-agent harnesses as a four-stage execution workflow covering goal interpretation, webpage perception, reasoning and planning, and action execution. Based on this abstraction, we build WebTrap Farm, a systematic and extensible evaluation framework covering three representative attack categories: malicious user p"

Existing approaches often rely on predefined records and fixed organizational rules, which cannot readily accommodate evolving tasks and interactions. This raises the question: can LLM agents organize their own memory using ordinary documents? We introduce self-organized memory (SoMem), a framework that stores memory in linked documents. The agent decides what to summarize, how to group related information, and when to revise existing topics, and every summary it produces stays linked to the original interactions as evidence. The runtime validates updates and automatically derives temporal, relational, and recent-memory views from the organized content. At query time, the agent c"

summary = "研究通过可执行设备日志区分答案与隐藏参考一致、证据充分及上下文行为适当性，揭示模型在可见历史不足时仍可能偶然答对却未能合理拒答。"

content = "研究构造两段合法完整历史，使其产生字节级相同的可见上下文却对应不同答案，以严格认证当前历史的证据不充分性。协议分别评估参考答案一致性、证据充分性和上下文适当行为，避免将偶然命中隐藏答案误判为记忆能力。在 203 个历史不足的原始前缀实例中，Qwen3-32B 命中 87 个隐藏参考但仅适当拒答 7 次，DeepSeek-V4-Flash 命中 85 个并拒答 21 次。紧凑 CSV 输入带来的适当性改善在两种模型上均无定论；独立评判器虽达到 99.38% 正确率，仍存在证据支持错误和截断问题，而确定性基线解决了全部构造案例。"
purpose = "- 为智能体记忆评测引入可认证的证据充分性标准，避免把不可判定上下文中的偶然正确答案计为能力提升。\n- 建议将参考答案匹配、合理拒答和证据支持程度拆分报告，而非仅使用最终答案准确率。\n- 可利用成对合法历史与字节级一致上下文，对模型是否依赖可见证据进行受控审计。\n- 结果主要诊断特定托管模型配置的评测失真，不应外推为通用记忆系统或自然语言证据判断能力的结论。"
notes = "标题：Correct Answers from Insufficient Histories: A Controlled Study of Agent Memory Evaluation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11840\n摘要：An answer can match the reference for a complete agent history even when the reader's visible history does not determine it. On 80 confirmation histories, Qwen3-32B matches 87 hidden references among 203 insufficient raw-prefix instances, with only seven appropriate abstentions. A fixed-input DeepSeek-V4-Flash replication matches 85 and abstains on 21 of the same 203 instances. Each reader makes 1,600 requests. Compact CSV's estimated appropriateness effects"

notes = "标题：Publications as benchmarks: auditing AI agents on spatial transcriptomics alignment\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11796\n摘要：AI agents are increasingly automating data analysis, but responsible adoption requires reliable performance evaluation, which is costly in specialized scientific domains. We introduce a framework that translates scientific publications into auditable benchmarks for AI agents and demonstrate its use on spatial transcriptomics alignment tasks. Across 121 tasks, 16 baseline methods, and six AI agents, the best agents (GPT-6-Astra and Gemini-4-Argon) matched or outperformed all baselines on 80% of tasks, often using surprisingly simple heuristics. Agent performance was robust to prompting and access to pre-installed packages. Benchmark construction also uncovered errors in prior work and shortcuts in current evaluation metrics. Finally, we show that agent-generated solutions can be distilled i"

content = "HolidayFactory 1.0 面向工业机器人重复作业，强调前序操作会持续改变场景状态，而非在每个评测回合开始时重置环境。框架包含 HOLIDAY-SIM、智能体化世界构建和 24/7-BENCH 三部分，覆盖接触与执行器仿真、可执行场景生成及任务评测。24/7-BENCH 提供七类工业任务，以完成、失败和未完成作业数，以及吞吐量、延迟和电机能耗衡量持续运行表现。实验验证了接触、执行器和触觉模型，并指出模型评判器及自动生成评测在忠实覆盖任务要求方面仍存在局限。"
purpose = "- 为工业机器人提供更贴近实际生产流程的持续状态评测范式，避免场景重置掩盖误差累积与连锁影响。\n- 可用于构建仿真就绪的工业环境、任务变体及可执行评测规则，降低具身任务开发成本。\n- 吞吐量、延迟、能耗和失败作业等指标有助于综合比较机器人策略的长期生产性能。\n- 自动生成的评测仍需人工确认与可执行检查，提示具身基准构建应重视任务语义和指标之间的一致性。"
notes = "标题：HolidayFactory 1.0: Simulating, Generating, and Evaluating Industrial Robot Tasks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11789\n摘要：Robots are typically evaluated in episodes that begin from a reset, while industrial work consists of repeated jobs whose outcomes affect subsequent operations. Evaluating such work at scale requires simulation of task-critical physical interactions, construction of industrial environments, and measurements of performance as scene state persists. We present HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0, a framework for simulating, generating, and evaluating industrial robot work. Given a human-specified workplace and task, the framework defines a world pairing a scenario of scene variants and process logic with an evaluation defining a job, metrics, and a rubric. HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0 comprises three components. HOLIDAY-SIM extends Newton's SDF-based hydroelastic contact with additional constitutive laws and a semi-imp"

summary = "研究发现，多智能体系统中的信息披露会形成行为传播通道，其集体福利效应取决于个体激励与群体目标是否一致，完全透明并非总是最优。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11714"
code = ""

content = "研究将“谁观察谁”的信息网络与智能体实际交互的网络解耦，把观察关系作为可独立优化的设计变量。实验让 10 个相同的 LLM 智能体参与六类重复群体决策游戏，共运行 1,080 个回合序列，并在固定预算下添加可查看同伴历史行动的有向连接。结果显示，观察关系会产生行为耦合：当个体行为与集体目标一致时，更多信息通常有益或影响中性；存在搭便车或拥堵外部性时，信息反而会放大有害行为，公共品博弈中不披露信息的表现最佳。在固定连接预算下，模块化的“杠铃型”拓扑会让局部群体固化有害行为，成为表现最差的网络结构。"
purpose = "- 将信息可见性和观察拓扑纳入多智能体系统设计，而不是默认采用完全透明的信息共享机制。\n- 在部署前评估个体激励与集体目标的对齐程度，据此决定信息披露量和连接方式。\n- 对存在搭便车、拥堵或负外部性的任务，应限制可能造成行为传染的观察链路，并警惕高度模块化的通信结构。\n- 可为多智能体编排、通信拓扑优化及群体涌现风险控制提供实验依据。"
notes = "标题：Steering Emergent Behavior in Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Information Networks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11714\n摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate in groups, each pursuing an individual objective while jointly determining system performance. If an operator controls which agent observes which other agent, but not their payoffs or messages, how should observations be structured to raise collective welfare? We make the information network (who observes whom) a design object, decoupled from the fixed interaction network over which agents act. Ten identical LLM agents play repeated group-decision games; by default each observes only an anonymous summary of the group's previous-round outcome, and a manager with a fixed budget adds directed observation links that reveal peers' past actions. Across 1,080 episodes and six games spanning coordination, anti-coordination, social dilemmas, an"

score = 3

content = "研究集成商用实验自动化仪器与AI决策模块，形成覆盖设计、构建、测试和学习周期的物理闭环测试床，并实现累计24小时无人值守运行。平台通过自主配制水性染料混合物，使其吸收光谱匹配指定目标，共完成6轮独立实验，每轮包含48次闭环迭代、持续约4小时。系统能够在无人干预下完成配方选择、液体分配和光谱测量，并依据实验结果持续生成下一组配方。对比结果显示，智能体优化器达到目标所需的迭代次数比贝叶斯优化少，最高降幅为52%，且成功解决了一个贝叶斯优化在48次迭代内未能完成的目标。"

We validate this platform on a minimal closed-loop discovery task in which the system autonomously formulates aqueous dye mixtures to match a target "

summary = "FinCutBench通过持续更新的中英双语金融问答与月度知识曲线，估计固定模型参数化金融知识的时间边界并揭示回测中的前视偏差。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11588"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "FinCutBench面向金融回测等时间点敏感任务，评估模型参数是否包含目标日期之后才公开的金融事实，从而识别参数化前视偏差。当前基准包含9,000道中英双语四选一题，时间覆盖2023年1月至2026年9月，涉及中国和美国市场及四类金融信息。研究按事实公开月份构建月度知识曲线，并在通过下降检验与结点检查后，将拟合曲线首次到达较低水平的月份定义为 FinCut Boundary。实验显示，不同模型的边界日期和定位精度存在差异，该边界还能为资产排序、财报记忆等下游评估划分时间窗口并辅助解释性能下降。"
purpose = "- 为金融回测和时间点任务提供模型参数化知识截止日期的经验参考，减少隐性前视偏差。\n- 支持依据 Transition Start 与 FinCut Boundary 划分评估样本，提升跨模型比较的时间有效性。\n- 持续按月更新题库，可用于监测模型金融知识覆盖范围及其随版本变化的漂移。\n- 提醒金融评测不能只限制外部检索数据日期，还需审计模型参数中可能提前包含的未来信息。"
notes = "标题：FinCutBench: A Continually Updated Benchmark for Measuring the Temporal Boundaries of LLM Financial Knowledge\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11588\n摘要：In financial backtesting and other point-in-time tasks, external inputs can be restricted by date, but model parameters may retain financial facts disclosed later, introducing parametric look-ahead bias. We introduce FinCutBench, a continually updated monthly financial knowledge benchmark for measuring how far a fixed model's financial knowledge extends over calendar time. The current dataset contains 9,000 Chinese and English four-option multiple-choice questions from January 2023 to September 2026, covering Chinese and U.S. markets and four categories of financial information. We construct monthly knowledge profiles using the approximate months in which the tested facts became public. After a profile passes the decline test and knot checks, we define the FinCut Boundary as the first mont"


[[items]]
id = "earn-llm"
title = "EARN：冻结 LLM 智能体的测试时适应与路径固化"
subtopic = "信用分配"

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "EARN 通过抑制重复得分动作的信用、奖励取得更高分的动作片段，并在末期条件回放有效路径，实现冻结 LLM 智能体的无参数测试时适应。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11565"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "EARN 面向参数冻结的 LLM 智能体，利用多轮交互反馈调整 LLM 所提议动作的选择概率，无需更新模型权重。它降低只能反复达到历史已有分数的动作信用，并为取得更高分数的动作片段分配更大信用，从而鼓励持续探索和性能突破。在交互预算的最后阶段，若当前上下文与历史记录匹配且动作仍可执行，系统会直接回放已存储动作序列，避免末期探索导致性能退化。EARN 在 Jericho、ScienceWorld 和 Crafter 的 33 个任务—预算设置中有 31 个取得最佳或并列最佳末期平均分，并在 Jericho 最后四轮中减少了 78.9%–86.3% 的候选动作生成调用。"
purpose = "- 为冻结智能体提供无需参数更新的在线适应方案，适合模型不可微调或部署成本受限的场景。\n- 将“突破历史最好成绩”纳入信用分配，可避免智能体持续强化仅能重复获得相同回报的动作。\n- 通过末期路径固化与条件回放平衡前期探索和后期利用，并降低候选动作生成成本。\n- 评估交互预算结束前多轮平均表现，有助于衡量智能体在固定预算下的稳定最终性能。"
notes = "标题：Actions Should EARN Their Credit: Test-Time Adaptation and Route Consolidation for Frozen LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11565\n摘要：LLM agents can use feedback from repeated attempts to improve their decisions without updating model parameters. However, positive action credits can continue to favor actions that repeatedly achieve the same score, even when higher scores are achievable. Under a fixed interaction budget, trying new actions in the final attempts can also lower performance. We introduce EARN, a method that uses past experience to guide further progress and retain earlier performance through conditional reuse of effective action sequences. EARN reduces action credits for reaching scores repeatedly attained in earlier episodes and assigns larger credits to action segments that reach higher scores. These credits adjust the probabilities of LLM-proposed actions. During the final episodes, EARN directly replays "

content = "LiveAsset是一种意图脚手架，针对用户目标与偏好未被充分说明、需要多轮纠正才能逐步明确的智能体应用场景。系统联合分析用户纠正、智能体原始结果和修订结果，推断隐含意图并增量更新可复用的意图资产，同时保留仍然适用的既有要求。执行阶段，这些要求从任务入口开始提供行为指导，并在每次回复交付前判断其上下文适用性、验证满足情况，驱动智能体修正未满足要求且避免破坏已满足要求。实验覆盖 π-Bench、PERMA、Vita 的同任务回放，以及 PAST-Bench、SkillEvolBench 的跨任务迁移，结果优于记忆、技能和规则型脚手架基线。"
purpose = "- 将用户纠正转化为可复用、可增量维护的意图资产，降低相似任务中反复沟通偏好的成本。\n- 通过交付前的适用性判断与执行验证，把用户意图从静态记忆提升为可直接干预运行过程的控制机制。\n- 为个性化智能体、办公自动化和在线服务智能体提供从交互反馈持续学习用户要求的实现路径。\n- 表明意图资产能够跨任务迁移，可作为长期记忆、技能库和规则系统之外的智能体脚手架组件。"
notes = "标题：LiveAsset: Learning Intent Assets from Corrective Interactions for Agent Runtime Control\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11560\n摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents acting across web applications and online services often need multi-turn corrections to align their work with users' underspecified goals and preferences. In practice, users may identify problems in the current outcome without specifying the desired result, so learning user intentions requires interpreting each correction against the agent's preceding and revised outcomes. These expectations are often connected to a particular context, making it hard to know when they apply and how to ensure the agent later acts as the user expects. To this end, we propose LiveAsset, an intent harness that learns reusable intent assets from corrective interactions and uses them for runtime control of agent execution. LiveAsset jointly interprets each correction with the ag"

notes = "标题：DriveFoundry: Co-Evolving Data and Model for Efficient Closed-Loop Iteration in Autonomous Driving\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11532\n摘要：A driving foundation model must learn reusable decision knowledge from rare critical events and apply it under strict runtime constraints. The Data Engine builds traceable scene-candidate records linking scene evidence and factor-decision relations to candidate trajectories and outcome assessments. Candidate comparisons and counterfactual annotations clarify how scene factors affect behavioral feasibility and preference. Verified model failures guide VLM-assisted annotation and expert correction, refining training data and annotation systems. The model engine, Xiaomi-Drive-o1, unifies Perception, Causatio"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "SimplEvo让多模态具身智能体从训练回合中自主撰写并迭代文本记忆，无需更新权重或后训练策略即可显著提升任务成功率，并实现跨场景与跨模型迁移。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11509"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SimplEvo使用一次示范生成与坐标无关的任务记忆，并仅基于训练回合构建容量受限的工作记忆。智能体在每个回合结束后总结操作流程、任务完成条件和失败恢复经验，测试时则固定两类记忆，避免测试信息泄漏。该方法无需权重更新、后训练策略或特权信息，使GPT-6-Astra在EBench上的成功率由46.7%提升至81.7%，并在RoboDojo取得69.63%的成功率。纯文本记忆以更少的词元达到接近视觉帧记忆的效果，还能泛化至未见布局和随机场景；由较强模型写出的工作记忆也可直接迁移给较弱模型。"
purpose = "- 展示无需参数更新的记忆演化可以成为提升具身智能体能力的轻量路径。\n- 为区分基础模型原生能力与复杂智能体脚手架带来的增益提供严格的训练—测试评估方案。\n- 说明结构化文本经验可替代高成本视觉轨迹存储，从而降低上下文与推理成本。\n- 跨模型复用工作记忆表明，智能体经验可以作为独立于模型权重的可迁移资产。"
notes = "标题：Review the Episodes, Refine the Lessons: Simple Memory Evolution for Embodied Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11509\n摘要：Recent studies of embodied agents based on frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) develop harness engineering around the MLLM, such as harnesses with post-trained policies and recursive self-improvement loops. Yet whether these additions exceed the native capability of the MLLM remains unexplored. We introduce SimplEvo to explore how far an MLLM can improve under direct control when it retains its execution experience in self-written text memories. It requires no weight updates, post-trained policies, or privileged information. The MLLM writes a coordinate-free task memory once from a single demonstration. Under a strict train-test protocol, it then creates a bounded working memory on training episodes only and refines it after each episode with lessons on procedure, completion,"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11499"
code = ""

content = "JoMoHa关注语言智能体中模型权重与执行脚手架相互制约的问题，指出朴素交替训练会造成二者逐步不兼容，干扰任务执行甚至损害模型训练。框架使用强化学习联合训练可执行脚手架生成策略和任务执行策略，使运行时结构与模型行为协同演化。JoMoHa在ALFWorld、WebShop和DBBench上优于仅优化权重或仅优化脚手架的基线，平均成功率达到70.3%，在ALFWorld上达到97.0%。系统能够从自身生成的轨迹中同时学习任务执行与运行时改进，形成递归自我改进闭环。"
purpose = "- 为模型权重与智能体脚手架的协同训练提供统一的强化学习框架，避免单独优化造成接口和行为失配。\n- 表明智能体性能提升不仅依赖模型参数，也取决于运行时脚手架能否随模型共同演化。\n- 可用于构建基于执行轨迹持续改进任务策略与脚手架生成策略的递归自我改进系统。\n- 为评估模型—框架协同优化提供ALFWorld、WebShop和DBBench等跨环境实验依据。"
notes = "标题：JoMoHa: Joint Model--Harness Optimization with Reinforcement Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11499\n摘要：The performance of language agents depends jointly on model weights and the harness. Optimizing either alone can be constrained by the other component, limiting overall performance gains. Our analysis of naive alternating training reveals progressive incompatibility between the model and harness, which increasingly interferes with task execution and may also undermine model training, leading to performance degradation. To coordinate the evolution of models and harnesses, we introduce JoMoHa, to our knowledge the first framework to jointly train an executable harness generation policy and a task execution policy through reinforcement learning. JoMoHa outperforms the evaluated weight-only and harness-only baselines and achieves the highest success rates among the evaluated methods on ALFWorl"

score = 3

summary = "SemanticKV 利用规划器提取的语义约束指导 KV 缓存保留，在无需训练的情况下显著降低智能体自回归视频生成的显存占用并提升推理速度。"

code = ""

link = ""
content = "SemanticKV 面向包含语义规划、序列生成和视觉验证的智能体自回归视频生成，解决长视频历史及候选轨迹导致的 KV 缓存膨胀问题。该方法通过文本交叉注意力定位与规划器语义约束相关的视频位置，再按约束对自注意力查询分组并计算缓存条目的保留分数。在给定显存预算下，它使用统一的 top-k 选择保留关键条目，同时保持键、值和位置不变，且无需重新训练或额外生成视频。在 Causal Forcing、0.7 压缩率设置下，SemanticKV 获得 84.10 的 VBench 总分，实现相对 Full KV 的 1.22 倍加速，并将 KV 缓存从 6.04 GB 降至 1.81 GB。"
purpose = "- 为长时自回归视频生成提供训练-free 的 KV 缓存压缩方案，降低候选轨迹扩展带来的显存和注意力计算成本。\n- 将智能体规划阶段的语义约束引入缓存选择，可优先保留支撑目标属性与动作的视觉状态。\n- 语义分组后的最坏组注意力损失上界为缓存筛选提供理论解释，并有助于兼顾多项生成约束。\n- 可作为不同自回归视频骨干上的独立推理优化模块，在基本维持生成质量的同时提升长视频生成效率。"
notes = "标题：Semantically Aligned KV Cache Compression for Efficient Agentic Autoregressive Video Generation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11452\n摘要：Agentic autoregressive video generation combines semantic planning, sequential generation, and visual verification for controllable synthesis. Growing visual histories enlarge the key-value (KV) cache, increasing memory use and attention costs across candidate rollouts. Existing compression methods primarily optimize individual generators without the planner's semantic constraints, risking low priority for states supporting requested attributes or actions. We introduce SemanticKV, a training-free method for semantically aligned KV cache compression. To our knowledge, SemanticKV is the first KV cache compression method for agentic autoregressive video generation that explicitly uses planner-extracted semantic constraints to guide retention. Text cross-attention identifies constraint-related"
