content = "BiVerify首先利用后向验证检查候选答案是否满足原始约束，再通过前向验证定位推理错误并判断目标是否完整。框架根据验证结果动态切换方向、重置状态和重新验证，持续修订候选答案或验证流程，直至同一答案同时通过两个方向。其共享数学工具将逻辑推理与精确计算分离，以提高复杂数学任务中的验证可靠性。该方法在五个高难度数学推理基准及多款DeepSeek、GPT和Gemini模型上接受评测，平均准确率较最强公开基线提高13.5个百分点，在GPT模型上的提升达到31.2个百分点。"
purpose = "- 将答案约束检查与推理过程检查结合，可弥补单向验证遗漏约束或无法保证过程正确的局限。\n- 动态方向切换、状态重置和重复验证可作为数学推理智能体的闭环自我纠错机制。\n- 逻辑推理与精确计算解耦的工具设计，有助于降低计算错误对验证结果的干扰。\n- 实验结果表明，组合互补验证器比单独增加某一方向的验证更具收益。"
notes = "标题：BiVerify: Closing the Loop Between Backward and Forward Verification for Mathematical Self-Correction\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11368\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) have made substantial progress in mathematical reasoning but still produce incorrect solutions to complex problems, indicating the need for more reliable self-correction mechanisms. Mathematical self-correction typically identifies errors through verification, which can proceed backward or forward. The two directions provide complementary verification capabilities. Backward verification checks a candidate answer against the original constraints, bypassing complex intermediate reasoning, but cannot ensure correct reasoning or objective completeness. Forward verification follows the problem conditions and current solution, enabling it to locate reasoning errors and assess objective completeness, but may overlook key constraints in complex solution chains. We prop"

purpose = "- 为冻结权重条件下的智能体自我改进提供更严格的对照设计，区分失败经验本身与搜索、筛选带来的收益。\n- 提示脚手架优化不能仅依据同分布局部决策成绩，还需在完整交互和新任务上验证经验迁移。\n- 强调开展智能体评测前应审计测试样本质量，避免缺陷题目造成虚假的改进或退化结论。\n- 表明中性笔记可能优于复杂的已学习经验，应将简单基线纳入提示与脚手架优化实验。"
notes = "标题：Agent Self-Improvement with Frozen Weights: The Added Value and Limits of Failure Traces\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11342\n摘要：We evaluate whether an AI agent's harness, the notes around its model, can learn from the agent's failure traces beyond a matched search that never sees them. It can, but less than it looks. We tested an open 9-billion-parameter model with fixed weights; a larger teacher model wrote its notes. On generated customer-service decisions, where both note writers were told the target rules, notes based on the agent's mistakes improved accuracy by 4.0 percentage points over notes from the same process with the mistakes withheld. That process alone, trying and keeping better-scoring notes, reached about 60% of the total improvement. In full customer-service conversations the same notes showed no demonstrated improvement. Separately, a strict screen of single lessons admitted two customer-service l"


[[items]]
id = "taca"

summary = "TACA通过优势投影、决策对比与监督回放，缓解智能体强化学习中负信用压制工具调用的问题，并在六项推理基准上稳定提升准确率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11330"
code = "https://github.com/tmlr-group/TACA"
dataset = ""

content = "论文发现，错误轨迹中的工具词元占比更高，会使工具调用获得整体负信用，导致训练过程中推理与工具使用能力下降。词元均值聚合会让较长的错误轨迹在策略更新中权重更大，而执行失败后的反复重试又进一步延长负信用影响范围。作者提出 Transition-Aware Credit Assignment（TACA），通过 Advantage Projection 保证同一提示组内工具调用词元的平均优势非负，并用 Decision Contrast 比较工具辅助回答与直接回答的奖励。TACA还利用 Supervised Replay 重放历史正确轨迹中的工具调用，在六项推理基准上相较 MT-GRPO 将 Qwen2.5-1.5B 和 Qwen2.5-3B 的平均准确率分别提升 4.61 和 3.61 个百分点。"
purpose = "- 揭示工具能力退化可能源于轨迹长度、词元分布与聚合方式共同造成的信用分配偏差。\n- 可用于改进带可验证奖励的智能体强化学习，避免策略更新系统性压制工具调用。\n- 提示训练系统应分别监控工具调用发起、参数生成、失败重试及恢复行为的优势信号。\n- 为结合对比奖励与正确轨迹回放来稳定工具使用提供了可复用方案。"
notes = "标题：When Credit Turns Against Tools: Transition-Aware Credit Assignment in Agentic Reinforcement Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11330\n摘要：Agentic reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards trains language models through multi-turn tool interactions, yet their reasoning performance and tool-calling ability can decline during training. We trace this degradation to three coupled mechanisms: (1) tool-token imbalance produces negative overall tool credit when incorrect trajectories contain more tool tokens per turn than correct ones; (2) aggregation amplification can further strengthen this negative signal as token-mean aggregation gives longer incorrect trajectories greater weight in policy updates; and (3) failure-driven retries prolong trajectories as models tend to call tools again after execution errors, extending potential negative credit to recovery attempts. To counter this suppression, we propose Transition-Aware Cre"


[[items]]
id = "zeropoint"
title = "ZeroPoint：轻量级能量决策模型"
subtopic = "置信度评估"
topics = [

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ZeroPoint通过为“空答案”设置固定零能量参考点，使能量模型能够在统一概率分布中完成候选选择与拒答决策。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12992"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "ZeroPoint将空答案纳入能量模型，并对所有输入固定其能量为零，从而为候选选项提供绝对兼容性参照。候选选项与空答案共享同一概率分布，模型可在常规推理中直接选择答案或拒答，无需额外阈值规则。其能量函数采用轻量级多语言编码器，可联合读取输入和全部候选项，并对候选顺序保持不变。消融实验表明，主要收益来自加入“无候选项适用”的训练样本；固定零点对可回答与不可回答输入的区分优于阈值化 Softmax，并略优于可学习拒答类别。"
purpose = "- 为选择题、候选排序和检索决策系统提供统一的选项选择与拒答机制。\n- 说明标准能量模型只有相对能量，若缺少固定参考点，难以可靠判断所有候选项是否均不合适。\n- 表明高质量负例或“无正确选项”样本对拒答能力训练至关重要。\n- 可用于降低强制回答带来的错误风险，同时保持候选项之间的原有选择能力。"
notes = "标题：ZeroPoint: Lightweight Energy-Based Decision Model\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12992\n摘要：Energy-based models (EBMs) assign a scalar energy to every joint value of the observed and predicted variables. In decision models, the prediction is chosen from a finite list of options provided with each input. Inference then reduces to comparing the energies of the options, and training lowers the energy of the correct option relative to the others. A complete decision must also account for the possibility that no option is acceptable. However, the energies learned by a standard EBM define only relative compatibility: adding the same input-dependent quantity to all energies leaves the predictions, the Gibbs distribution, and the usual training losses unchanged. The model therefore has no learned reference level against which to decide whether any option fits. We propose ZeroPoint, an EB"

id = "verkv-kv-cache"
title = "VerKV：先验证后取回的可靠 KV Cache 压缩"
subtopic = "缓存优化"
topics = [

    "推理加速",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "VerKV 通过 GPU 端关键 token、全缓存草图和按查询验证取回机制，为注意力近似提供可控误差保证，同时显著降低显存占用与 CPU-GPU 数据传输。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11319"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "VerKV 将完整 KV Cache 保留在 CPU 内存，仅在 GPU 上维护查询无关的关键 token 及全缓存的 token 级草图。其 PoissonComp 随机采样程序用于选择关键 token，KVFetch 则在推理时估计注意力计算的相对误差。若估计结果满足用户指定的 $(\\epsilon,\\delta)$ 容差，系统无需访问主机内存；否则按当前查询从 CPU 补取 token，以逐步满足误差要求。在 Qwen3-4B、Qwen3.5-9B 和 Ministral-3-8B 的 32K 上下文实验中，该方案达到稠密注意力 90%–100% 的质量，将 GPU 显存占用降低 8–32 倍，并把每个生成 token 的缓存取回比例控制在 2%–23%。"
purpose = "- 为长上下文、长期运行智能体和多智能体系统提供具有误差保证的 KV Cache 压缩方案。\n- 以“先验证、再取回”替代固定压缩率策略，在输出质量、显存占用和传输开销之间动态权衡。\n- token 级草图与按查询检索机制可用于构建更可靠的分层缓存和 CPU-GPU 异构推理系统。\n- $(\\epsilon,\\delta)$ 容差接口使缓存近似精度可配置，便于面向不同延迟与可靠性要求部署。"
notes = "标题：Verify Before You Fetch: Reliable KV Cache Compression for LLM Inference\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11319\n摘要：Long-context applications, long-lived agents, and multi-agent systems require large language models (LLMs) to process and retain extensive information. In these settings, the KV cache can exceed GPU high-bandwidth memory (HBM) capacity, necessitating offloading to host memory, where CPU-to-GPU transfers can dominate inference latency. While KV cache compression can reduce data movement, query-agnostic compression that guarantees accurate attention for arbitrary queries is extremely challenging, if not impossible. We introduce VerKV, a hybrid approach that provides $(ε, δ)$ guarantees for approximating attention on any given query. VerKV maintains a compact set of query-agnostic pivotal tokens on the GPU, together with a token-level sketch of the full KV cache, while retaining the complete "

summary = "NCCL-BOT以分析成本模型作为先验，并通过在线多臂老虎机根据实测延迟持续选择集合通信算法与协议，显著减少AUTO策略的高损失场景。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11303"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "NCCL的AUTO策略依赖分析成本模型选择集合通信的算法—协议组合，但不利用实际测得的延迟，因此会在少数上下文中产生严重误判。NCCL-BOT以该成本模型进行热启动，为每类上下文维护在线多臂老虎机，并依据运行时延迟持续调整选择。在H100离线回放实验中，损失超过10%的上下文由19个降至1个，p99选择损失由103%降至9.9%，平均损失由6.1%降至1.6%。其在GB200上的迁移结果与在线实验表现并不完全稳定，且小于1 MiB的消息会受到插件开销影响，论文尚未证明端到端净收益。"
purpose = "- 展示了将解析成本模型用作强先验、再以在线反馈修正长尾错误的系统优化路径。\n- 表明持续探索优于冷启动或每个候选仅探测一次后固定决策，适合运行环境动态变化的通信库。\n- 提醒部署时应按消息规模设置回退机制，小消息可继续交由原始成本模型处理以规避插件开销。\n- 跨GPU代际和网络拓扑的收益仍需独立校准，并应通过端到端工作负载验证实际加速效果。"
notes = "标题：NCCL-BOT: Closing the Loop on NCCL's Cost Model with Online Bandits\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11303\n摘要：NCCL picks an algorithm-protocol pair for every collective from an analytic cost model (AUTO) that never sees a measured latency. The model is a strong prior but a brittle final decision: priced on forced-arm runs, its choice is exact on most contexts and badly wrong in a few. We built NCCL-BOT, a tuner plugin that closes the loop: a per-context bandit, warm-started from the model, learns from measured latencies. We replay a forced-arm H100 sweep, tuned at 4-8 nodes and scored at 16-32, and price the model's choice on the same forced arms; that sweep has no stock-NCCL run. The bandit cuts the contexts where the model's choice loses more than 10% from 19 to 1 (p99 103% to 9.9%, mean 6.1% to 1.6%; p=0.047 over nine scenarios after Holm correction). The prior and continued learning both matte"

topics = [

summary = "Third Eye 通过加入后续原生更新与独立评分，发现模型自更新的即时收益可能无法延续，甚至会在再次更新后显著转负。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12988"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出 Third Eye，将自更新的即时表现与模型再次原生更新后的持续效用分开评估。实验覆盖五个 3B–8B 参数骨干模型及数学、代码任务，共进行 64 次独立运行，并为每次运行评估三个候选更新及其三个后续更新。平衡比较中，即时选择效用接近于零，但继续更新后效用下降 1.78 个百分点；SmolLM3-3B 和 Llama-3.1-8B 也出现下降，而 Qwen3-8B 的结果尚不确定。目标任务与分布外任务的损失是主要退化来源，匹配更新配方及受体—供体控制实验均未找到可靠缓解方案或单一退化机制。"
purpose = "- 评估递归自我改进时，不应只报告单次更新后的即时性能，还需测量后续更新产生的持续影响。\n- 候选选择与最终评分应使用互不重叠的数据，并设置不更新基线，以降低选择偏差。\n- 可将持久化自更新建模为连续的适应状态转移，同时监测目标任务、分布外能力与能力保持情况。\n- 结果提示自我更新系统需要面向多步稳定性设计验证与停止机制，而非以局部性能提升作为唯一依据。"
notes = "标题：Third Eye: Beyond Immediate Gains in Recursive Self-Improvement\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12988\n摘要：Does a useful self-update remain useful after the model updates again? Third Eye separates immediate performance from the outcome of a further native update. We evaluate five 3-8B backbones on math and code: 64 fresh runs, three candidates per run, and three continuations per candidate. Selection and final scoring use disjoint items. In the balanced Qwen3-4B/Llama-3.2-3B comparison, immediate-selected utility is near zero, but continuation reduces it by 1.78 percentage points (simultaneous 95% interval [0.68, 2.88]). Candidate-averaged immediate utility is already negative: selection partly accounts for the apparently neutral first step. Continuation also lowers utility for SmolLM3-3B and Llama-3.1-8B; Qwen3-8B remains inconclusive. Target and out-of-distribution losses dominate retention "

score = 3

summary = "论文以完整解支持度和原子技能覆盖度构建二维框架，用于预测基础模型经强化学习后训练后是放大已有能力、学会技能组合，还是受限于能力缺失。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11260"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出后训练可行性的二维状态空间：有效完整解支持度衡量在可行探索预算内能否采样到成功轨迹，原子技能覆盖度衡量模型能否独立执行解题所需的组成技能。两项指标将基础模型划分为能力放大型、组合受限型和习得受限型三种状态，并对应不同的经验特征与训练干预。基于三个小语言模型的合成组合任务实验表明，掌握全部原子技能并不意味着强化学习必然能学会组合；若训练期间始终采样不到完整解，性能可能无法提升。少量组合监督可建立非零的完整解支持度，进而带来显著的强化学习收益，因此中期训练可被视为改善后训练初始状态的准备阶段。"
purpose = "- 在投入强化学习训练前，通过完整解采样率与原子技能测试判断任务是否具备可学习性。\n- 区分技能缺失与组合探索失败，避免对不同瓶颈统一采用增加强化学习预算的策略。\n- 对组合受限任务先加入少量组合监督或课程学习，使成功轨迹进入策略分布的有效支持集。\n- 将中期训练设计为后训练准备阶段，有针对性地补齐原子技能并提高完整解的非零采样概率。"
notes = "标题：Primitives and Paths: Predicting What Post-Training Can Do from What the Base Model Already Has\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11260\n摘要：Does reinforcement learning teach language models new capabilities, or merely amplify ones acquired during pre-training? The literature offers evidence for both: pass@$k$ studies suggest RL reweights latent behaviours while some controlled composition studies demonstrate gains beyond simple amplification. We argue that this question has no answer independent of the capability state of the base model, and propose a two-dimensional state space for post-trainability. The first axis, \\emph{effective full-solution support}, measures whether successful trajectories can be sampled under a feasible exploration budget. The second axis, \\emph{atomic primitive coverage}, measures whether the constituent skills of a solution can be executed when the burden of composition is removed. Together, these di"


[[items]]
id = "llm-5"
title = "LLM 智能体评测的回放保真度：随机性、隐藏状态与测试预言机"
subtopic = "评测审计"
topics = [
    "智能体评估",
    "模型评估",
    "轨迹审计",
    "状态追踪",
    "可复现性",
]
research = [
    "EvalEvolve",
    "HarnessEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "评测审计",
    "轨迹审计",
    "状态追踪",
    "可复现性",
    "测试预言机",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过审计 AppWorld 中 71 次失败执行，揭示随机数状态、隐藏物化状态和测试预言机集成方式会显著影响 LLM 智能体评测的回放结果与可复现性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11216"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究对固定历史 AppWorld 语料中的 71 次失败执行开展了 284 次隔离、代码固定的回放实验。仅重建显式配置时，按照输出、数据库增量及原生判定等检查只能复现 42 个案例；若在每轮交互前重置随机数生成器，则可复现案例增至 68 个。改用数据库和测试预言机检查后，两种设置间的差距由 26 例缩小至 4 例，说明评测结论高度依赖回放时控制和比较的状态范围。机制分析还发现，即使输出与判定一致，底层数据库行效应、JWT 凭证及认证写入状态仍可能不同；保留输出追踪器的原生导出则可修复全部 10 个受影响的预言机后导出案例。作为对照，12 个 BFCL 案例在所测试的公开状态、返回值和判定检查下未出现差异。"
purpose = "- 为智能体调试、失败分析和反事实评估建立更严格的回放保真度标准。\n- 回放系统应显式保存并恢复随机数生成器、认证凭证、数据库副作用及其他隐藏状态，而不能只比较文本输出。\n- 评测审计应同时采用输出、物化状态、数据库增量和原生测试预言机等多层检查，避免将表面一致误判为执行等价。\n- 设计可复现评测基础设施时，应保留原生输出追踪与导出路径，并如实记录资源删失和基础设施偏差。"
notes = "标题：Replay Fidelity in LLM Agent Evaluation: Randomness, Hidden State, and Test Oracles in AppWorld\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11216\n摘要：Reproducibility in large language model (LLM) agent evaluation depends on replay fidelity: which parts of an execution trace are reconstructed, controlled, and compared. This matters for agent debugging, failure analysis, and counterfactual evaluation of stateful tool use. We audit all 71 unsuccessful executions in a fixed historical AppWorld corpus using 284 isolated fixed-code replay attempts. Reconstructed declared settings admit 42 cases under printed-output, pre-oracle database-delta, and native-verdict/test-count checks; resetting the random-number generator before each interaction admits 68. The 26-case gap contracts to four under database/oracle checks, with one endpoint unknown. Mechanism studies identify four materialized-state discrepancies despite matching outputs and verdicts."


[[items]]
id = "uapm"
title = "UAPM：从未来交互学习的不确定性感知动态用户画像"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "UAPM通过Profile、Buffer与Rulebook分离已确认画像和歧义证据，并利用未来交互开展特权在策略蒸馏，在固定输入预算下提升动态个性化效果。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11204"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "UAPM将用户状态划分为Profile、Buffer和Rulebook：Profile保存有证据支持的通用或情境特征，Buffer暂存尚未确认的歧义行为，Rulebook记录用户特定的解释与更新规则。该设计避免把一次含义不明的新行为直接写入用户画像，并允许后续交互为历史证据提供解释。训练方法POPD-DS先将更强模型蒸馏为仅观察当前信息的Student和可利用未来信息的Teacher，再由固定Teacher以未来交互指导Student自身产生的画像管理决策。三个Web个性化任务的实验表明，在相同下游输入预算下，UAPM相较最佳同预算基线最高提升5%。"
purpose = "- 为动态用户画像提供“确认信息—待定证据—更新规则”分层管理机制，降低歧义行为造成的画像污染。\n- 展示如何仅在训练阶段利用未来交互作为特权信息，使部署阶段无需访问未来数据也能改进状态更新决策。\n- 可用于推荐、搜索和个性化智能体的长期用户建模，在有限上下文预算下保留更可靠的用户特征。\n- POPD-DS为带有延迟反馈的状态管理任务提供了Teacher指导与任务奖励结合的训练范式。"
notes = "标题：Not Yet in the Profile: Uncertainty-Aware Dynamic User Profiling by Learning from Future Interactions\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11204\n摘要：On the Web, user profiling builds compact user representations from interaction histories for efficient personalization, and dynamic user profiling updates them as users' behavior and preferences change. However, new user behavior can be ambiguous, and its interpretation differs across users. To address this, we introduce Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Profile Manager (UAPM), which separates the claims in a user profile from interpretations of new behavior that are not yet confirmed, through a Profile, a Buffer, and a Rulebook. UAPM records user-specific interpretation and update rules in the Rulebook, keeps ambiguous evidence in the Buffer outside the Profile as evidence for later decisions, and writes supported general and situational characteristics to the Profile. We train UAPM with Privil"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文揭示多数投票奖励下的测试时强化学习会在训练早期因采样噪声形成答案锁定，并利用参数核与初始 logits 闭式预测最终锁定结果。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11165"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文研究利用多数投票奖励在无标签问题上开展测试时强化学习时，模型为何会快速锁定某个答案，且在困难问题上可能锁定错误答案。作者指出，最终结果主要由训练初期相近候选答案之间受采样噪声驱动的短暂竞争决定，并推导了各候选答案胜出的概率。对于共享网络参数，其他候选答案的更新会通过参数核改变领先两个答案的 logit 间隔，因此可结合初始 logits 与核探测结果计算修正后的锁定概率。在两个模型家族、三个模型的 LoRA 实验中，训练前冻结核所得预测优于根据训练结果重新拟合的逻辑回归基线，但闭式规律依赖领先两个答案始终高于其他候选的条件。"
purpose = "- 可在实际训练前预测问题最终会锁定到哪个答案，提前识别高风险的错误自强化样本。\n- 说明多数投票测试时强化学习的成败可能在早期即由采样噪声决定，为设计延迟更新、增加探索或置信度门控机制提供依据。\n- 参数核刻画了共享参数更新在候选答案间产生的溢出效应，可用于改进测试时训练的稳定性分析。\n- 闭式预测无需拟合额外常数，可作为多数投票奖励训练的低成本诊断工具。"
notes = "标题：The Race Is Decided Early! A Kernel Law Predicts Lock-In in Majority-Vote Test-Time RL\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11165\n摘要：Test-time reinforcement learning with majority-vote rewards trains a language model on unlabeled questions by rewarding the samples that agree with its most frequent answer. During training, each question locks into one answer, on hard questions often a wrong one, but which answer it locks into is known only after training. In this paper, we show that this outcome is decided early, in a short race driven by sampling noise between the answers that are close at the start, and we derive the probability of each outcome. With a separate logit for each answer, the runner-up wins with a normal tail probability of the initial gap, scaled by an explicit noise constant. In a network, however, the answer logits depend on shared parameters, and through the kernel of these parameters the updates of the"

content = "研究以配对实验分离提交要求、既有草稿和候选答案对数学解题表现的影响。允许继续推导的指令在 390 次短输出调用中未产生有效最终答案，而单行提交指令产生了 390 个格式有效输出，其中 389 个可解析。在固定提交协议下，同时提供失败草稿和候选答案时正确率达到 59.60%，明显高于仅给问题的 9.09%；固定候选时草稿贡献 13.13 个百分点，固定草稿时候选贡献 25.76 个百分点。保留草稿尾部片段比保留长度相近的头部片段高 10.10 个百分点，表明失败输出仍包含可复用信息，但其价值取决于上下文组合与保留位置。"
purpose = "- 说明数学推理评测不仅测量模型能力，也会受到答案提交协议和输出格式约束的显著影响。\n- 提示可通过复用失败草稿、提供候选答案及优先保留尾部推导来改善二次作答。\n- 设计推理系统时应分别评估生成、草稿保留、候选选择和最终提交环节，避免将执行路径差异误判为能力差异。\n- 候选答案与草稿的收益具有条件性和次可加性，组合策略需要通过受控消融实验确定。"
notes = "标题：Task presentation and execution paths shape LLM mathematical performance\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11102\n摘要：Mathematical performance depends on how a language model receives information, uses generated text and submits an answer. On 65 development problems, an instruction permitting continued derivation produced no valid final answers in 390 short-output calls; a single-line submission instruction produced 390 format-valid outputs, including 389 parseable answers. Under the fixed submission protocol, 198 problems with failed initial submissions were answered correctly in 9.09p of cases with the question alone, 33.84p with its draft, 46.46p with candidates, and 59.60p with both. Drafts added 13.13 percentage points with candidates fixed, and candidates added 25.76 "

title = "Think or Remember：智能体推理中的思考与记忆成本交换率"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文建立与实际推理引擎计数一致的成本模型，揭示上下文长度、注意力开销与服务端缓存复用共同决定智能体保留或丢弃历史推理的成本及效果。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10982"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文将智能体推理成本拆分为按处理词元计费的成本 $c$ 与按注意力词元对计费的成本 $\\beta$，并定义交换率 $P^*=c/\\beta$。当上下文短于该阈值时，词元处理成本占主导，系统处于“思考”区间；超过阈值后，注意力成本占主导，进入“记忆”区间。基于 Qwen3-8B 的 ScienceWorld 轨迹实验显示，是否复用已生成状态会反转保留历史与删除早期推理的相对成本。HotpotQA 在线复现实验进一步表明，删除历史推理虽能降低点估计成本，却会使四个模型的正确率下降 9 至 23 个百分点。"
purpose = "- 为智能体长程执行中的上下文保留、历史裁剪和缓存策略提供可精确核算的成本模型。\n- 提醒系统设计者同时区分策略变更的机械成本与其改变生成行为后产生的行为成本。\n- 说明上下文策略不能脱离推理架构和服务端缓存机制单独比较，部署配置可能直接反转成本结论。\n- 为在任务准确率与推理成本之间选择历史压缩策略提供量化依据。"
notes = "标题：Think or Remember: The Exchange Rate of Agentic Inference\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10982\n摘要：Agents built on reasoning models resend their growing transcript at every call. Two transacting parties, not the model, decide what that costs: the client keeps or edits messages; the server decides whether earlier reasoning returns in the prompt and what its cache reuses. We define a cost model whose counts match an instrumented engine exactly; the architecture charges $c$ per token processed and $β$ per pair attended. Their ratio $P^*=c/β$ is an exchange rate between thinking and remembering: per token, processing dominates below this context length, a thinking regime, and attention above it, a memory regime. A policy change has a mechanical cost, incurred even if the agent writes the same tokens, and a behavioral one: it writes different tokens, changing cost and outcome. Serving alone "

id = "ai-lean"
title = "AI 引导的 Lean 证明精化改进解析常数界"

score = 3

summary = "该研究以部分形式化的 Lean 证明蓝图约束 AI 智能体迭代收紧中间引理，获得可解释、可验证的 Grothendieck 常数与 Korenblum 常数新界。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10961"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "研究将定义、引理和定理组织为部分形式化的 Lean 证明蓝图，以人类可读的依赖图保留原证明结构。AI 智能体在不重建完整论证的情况下迭代精化 Lean 代码，收紧中间引理中的松弛，并使局部改进自动传播至最终定理。该框架允许研究者检查每次修改及其对最终界的影响，兼顾数学发现的可解释性和机器验证。实验将 Grothendieck 常数上界改进为 $\\mathcal{K}_G \\leq \\frac{\\pi}{2\\log(1+\\sqrt{2})}-10^{-17}$，并将 Korenblum 常数下界提高至 $\\mathcal{K}_K \\geq 0.356565$。"
purpose = "- 展示部分形式化证明可作为人类数学论证与 AI 自动精化之间的可解释接口。\n- 利用证明依赖图定位并收紧中间引理中的松弛，避免从头搜索完整证明。\n- 为 AI 驱动的数学发现提供可由 Lean 检验的闭环，降低错误结果进入最终定理的风险。\n- 说明局部证明优化能够系统性传播并产生新的定量数学结果。"
notes = "标题：Improving Analytic Constants via Interpretable AI-Guided Lean Proof Refinement\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10961\n摘要：AI agents are increasingly capable of discovering new mathematical results, yet their reasoning and generated proofs can be difficult to understand and verify. We introduce an interpretable framework for AI-guided mathematical discovery, focused on improving quantitative bounds on analytic constants. Our key insight is that existing proofs often contain slack in intermediate lemmas, which can be tightened without reconstructing the entire argument. To exploit this structure, we represent proofs as partially-formalized Lean blueprints: human-interpretable dependency graphs of definitions, lemmas, and theorems. An AI agent then iteratively refines the Lean code while preserving the blueprint's dependency structure, allowing local improvements to automatically propagate to a verified final bo"

content = "论文指出，人机互补性通常以真实标签为参照，衡量人类在 AI 辅助下能否超越人类或 AI 单独行动。对于医疗治疗建议等非预测任务，输出质量取决于实践目标和多个评价维度，真实标签并不是恰当的评估基准。作者提出受医疗多准则决策分析启发的两步评分方法，以情境相关的适当性标签替代真实标签。该框架显式呈现参考标准构建中的规范性选择，并借鉴计算可靠主义分析支持标签构建的技术、科学与社会因素。"
purpose = "- 为缺乏客观真实标签的决策支持系统提供可量化的人机互补性评估框架。\n- 提醒评估者显式披露适当性标签背后的价值判断、目标权重与情境假设。\n- 可用于医疗决策等多目标场景，比较人类、AI 以及人机协作方案的实际效用。\n- 为人机协同评估的测量效度和参考标准构建提供方法论基础。"
notes = "标题：Complementarity without ground truth?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10893\n摘要：Human-AI complementarity captures the idea that a human assisted by an AI system can outperform both the human and the AI acting alone. In prediction tasks, this improvement is assessed against ground truth labels produced through a reference construction procedure and intended to represent the state of affairs to be predicted. Many human-AI interactions, including those where medical AI provides treatment recommendations, however, concern tasks other than predicting what obtains. Their relevant output concerns adequacy relative to a practical goal and depends on several evaluative dimensions. In this work, we examine the ground truth dependence of complementarity and extend it to human-AI interactions where ground truth is not the appropriate reference. We show that tasks beyond predictio"

summary = "该书系统分析生成长度、评分方式、量化、批处理、注意力后端与缓存等推断设置如何显著影响推理模型的评测结果、可复现性及服务成本。"

content = "作者指出，推理模型的实际表现不仅取决于权重，也高度依赖生成限制、答案抽取和评分方式等推断配置；仅调整最大响应长度，就曾使数学基准准确率从 0.08% 变为 93.18%。即使关闭采样，批次组成、注意力后端和缓存机制仍可能改变输出，给结果复现与系统公平比较带来挑战。书中进一步讨论批处理、显存容量、延迟、吞吐量和成本之间的权衡，以及量化对评测和部署的影响。最后覆盖结构化输出、工具调用、推理预算和长上下文任务，并给出记录推断运行、核查评测流程和估算服务容量的实践建议。"
purpose = "- 为推理模型评测提供配置审计清单，避免把生成长度或评分实现差异误判为能力差异。\n- 指导部署团队综合优化批处理、缓存、显存、延迟与吞吐量，而非只比较单一性能指标。\n- 强调完整记录推断参数、运行环境和后端实现，以提高实验可复现性与系统比较公平性。\n- 帮助研究者审慎解释推理预算、工具调用和长上下文基准所能支持的结论。"
notes = "标题：Inference for Reasoning Models: Practical Lessons from Evaluation and Serving\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10845\n摘要：Running a language model involves more than its trained weights. The same model can receive very different benchmark scores, produce different answers, or cost more to serve depending on how it is evaluated and run. In one experiment in this book, changing only the maximum response length moved accuracy on a math benchmark from 0.08% to 93.18%. This book explains how inference settings shape the results we report and the systems we deploy. We begin with evaluation, including generation limits, answer extraction, scoring methods, and quantization. We then examine why outputs can change across batches, attention backends, and caching, even when sampling is disabled. The next chapters cover serving performance, including batching, memory capacity, latency, throughput, and cost. We also study "

content = "本文从后训练基础出发，逐步讲解 GRPO 及完整强化学习训练循环。作者通过数学推理、搜索智能体、训练稳定性、混合专家模型和软件工程任务五类训练项目，复盘故障、调整措施及经验。重点分析无有效奖励、奖励作弊、探索坍缩和误导性评估等典型失效模式，并讨论强化学习对模型内部能力的实际影响。后续章节还覆盖奖励设计、工具型智能体训练、复现、 rollout 引擎与硬件配置，可作为开放模型 RL 后训练的实践参考。"
purpose = "- 为 RLVR 与 GRPO 后训练提供系统化的故障诊断清单。\n- 帮助识别奖励上升但真实能力未改善的奖励作弊和评估失真现象。\n- 为数学推理、搜索智能体及软件工程任务的奖励设计与训练调试提供参考。\n- 支持研究者在训练稳定性、可复现性、rollout 基础设施和硬件配置之间进行权衡。"
notes = "标题：What Broke & Why: Practical Lessons from Reinforcement Learning Post-Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10844\n摘要：Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an important way to improve how language models reason, solve problems, and use tools. But getting it to work in practice is not always straightforward. Training can fail because a model receives no useful reward, learns to exploit the reward system, stops exploring, or appears to improve when the evaluation itself is misleading. In this book, we give a practical introduction to reinforcement learning post-training for language models, focusing on what works, what fails, and why. We begin with the foundations of post-training, explain GRPO and the training loop step by step, and then follow five training programs covering math reasoning, search agents, training stability, mixture-of-experts models, and software engineering ta"

content = "研究将绘图到代码生成从一次性生成或验证器筛选，转化为“先生成、再定位错误并修复”的测试时迭代流程。作者把生成错误划分为四类，并训练轻量级判别器，结合代码及其渲染结果识别和定位问题。Visual-Code Diagnostics 不依赖传统编译器或调试器，而是以统一诊断系统提供视觉反馈，指导语义与视觉层面的代码修正。实验显示，该方法可稳定提升不同生成器的输出质量，并显著超过强测试时扩展基线。"
purpose = "- 为绘图代码生成提供贴近人类编程流程的测试时纠错机制。\n- 利用渲染结果与代码的联合诊断，实现错误识别、定位和定向修复。\n- 轻量级诊断器可作为外部反馈模块接入不同代码生成器，降低替换基础模型的成本。\n- 说明测试时计算不仅可用于候选采样与筛选，也可用于结构化的迭代代码优化。"
notes = "标题：Simple Test-Time Refinement for Plot-to-Code Generation via Visual-Code Diagnostics\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10840\n摘要：Plot-to-code generation has been greatly advanced by recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing studies mainly focus on one-pass generation or iterative sampling guided by verifier-based filtering, which does not align with the typical human programming workflow of generate first, then iteratively error-location and fix. This naturally motivates a test-time refinement paradigm for plot-to-code generation. In this work, we present the first study on plot-to-code generation via iterative test-time refinement. We propose a Visual-Code Diagnostics framework that enables refinement through visual feedback from source code. Specifically, we first categorize generation errors into four coarse-grained types, and then train lightweight discriminators to recognize and localiz"

summary = "研究以统一零样本协议评测67个系统在阿拉伯语任务上的表现，发现总体排名较稳定，但Jev在现代标准阿拉伯语与不同方言间存在显著且任务相关的性能差距。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10802"
code = ""

content = "研究采用冻结的阿拉伯语评测套件，以统一零样本协议比较47个决策模型和20个LLM，覆盖9类共43项任务。Jev 1.14.0的任务主指标均值为69.24，高于Jev 1.13.0的68.77，但低于排名第一的Gemma-4-31B（71.81）。移除英语对照项、跨任务重复项或同时移除两者后，系统总体排序保持不变。内容匹配分析显示，Jev 1.14.0在方言上的准确率相对现代标准阿拉伯语低0.35至7.94个百分点，且不同数据集和方言的差异较大。作者强调，聚合分数不能单独用于判断方言鲁棒性，还需结合任务族结果、覆盖范围及匹配语言对照。"

notes = "标题：Evaluating Jev on Arabic: Comparing Decision Models and LLMs Across Arabic Tasks and Dialects\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10802\n摘要：How does Jev compare with other systems on Arabic tasks, and how does its performance change between Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) and dialects? We evaluate 67 systems, including 47 decision models and 20 large language models (LLMs), on 43 tasks across 9 task families using a shared zero-shot protocol. The Arabic-focused frozen suite includes English controls in three parallel resources, which we also remove in a separate descriptive comparison. Jev 1.14.0 achieves a mean task-primary metric of 69.24 on a scale multiplied by 100, compared with 68.77 for Jev 1.13.0 and 71.81 for Gemma-4-31B, the highest-scoring system on this aggregate. Removing the recorded English controls, repeated cross-task items or both leaves the full system order unchanged. Relative performance and gains between Jev"

id = "trust"
title = "TRUST：具备统计保证的可信递归自我改进"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "TRUST通过在线多重假设检验控制递归自我改进中的错误演化率，在减少劣质更新的同时保持智能体性能增长。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10787"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出Trustworthy Recursive Updates with Statistical Testing（TRUST），用于可靠筛选智能体在递归自我改进过程中生成的脚手架更新。该框架将在线多重假设检验引入跨轮次更新决策，并由LLM结合历史上下文动态判断接受新版本所需的证据强度。理论上，TRUST能够限制已接受更新中造成不可接受性能损失的比例，并给出最终性能增益的下界。在HotpotQA、DROP和APPS上的实验显示，TRUST将劣质更新的平均接受比例从贪心门控的45.59%降至2.78%，且最终得分优于现有统计门控基线。"
purpose = "- 为递归自我改进系统提供具有统计保证的更新验收机制，避免验证集随机波动被误判为真实进步。\n- 可用于智能体脚手架、提示或工具链的持续演化，在无需重新训练底层LLM的情况下安全积累改进。\n- 表明在线多重假设检验可以在抑制错误演化与保留有益更新之间取得更好的平衡。\n- 为自演化系统建立可解释的可靠性指标与性能增益保证。"
notes = "标题：Trustworthy Recursive Self-Improvement with Statistical Guarantees\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10787\n摘要：Recursive self-improvement offers a promising path toward AI agents that improve through experience by refining their own harnesses without retraining their underlying large language models (LLMs). Making this self-improvement process reliable, however, requires more than generating new updates: agents must decide which changes are worth keeping. A greedy gate that accepts updates based solely on higher validation scores can mistake an observed gain for genuine progress and lead to performance degeneration, whereas overly conservative statistical gates may reject beneficial updates and hinder performance improvement. We introduce Trustworthy Recursive Updates with Statistical Testing (TRUST), a framework that adapts an online multiple hypothesis testing procedure to control the rate of fal"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "REINS通过动力学模型约束、前向验证和索引规则记忆，使LLM规划器在不修改权重的情况下持续积累经验，并以毫秒级延迟完成实时轨迹规划。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10748"
code = "https://github.com/ZhihongCui/REINS"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "REINS是一种面向实时轨迹规划的自进化智能体脚手架，旨在解决LLM在线决策延迟过高以及离线规划器无法持续演化的问题。系统将LLM输出约束到学习得到的变量级动力学模型，并通过校准谓词与前向滚动进行验证。通过验证的规划结果会被固化到索引式规则记忆中，后续相似场景可在不再次调用LLM的情况下以 O(log n) 复杂度检索执行。实验中，REINS实现了超过98%的合规率、近50%的碰撞减少和1.9至4.6毫秒延迟，且LLM调用率会随记忆成熟而持续下降。"
purpose = "- 为实时控制场景提供兼顾LLM规划能力与毫秒级执行预算的智能体运行时架构。\n- 展示无需更新LLM权重、仅通过验证与规则记忆固化即可实现闭环自进化的路径。\n- 可将“新场景调用LLM、验证后写入记忆、熟悉场景直接检索”的机制迁移到自动驾驶、机器人控制等长程决策任务。\n- 动力学约束和前向滚动验证可降低未经验证的语言模型输出直接进入控制环路的安全风险。"
notes = "标题：REINS: A Self-Evolving Agent Harness for Real-Time Trajectory Planning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10748\n摘要：Agent harnesses -- runtime scaffolds that wrap an LLM's reasoning loop with tool dispatch, verification, and persistent memory -- have become the standard substrate for deploying LLMs as autonomous agents, as demonstrated by Claude Code and Codex in software engineering. In trajectory planning, however, this paradigm has not taken hold: existing LLM-based trajectory planning methods either keep the LLM in the decision loop, exceeding the millisecond-level control budget, or offload it to offline rules or training-time supervision -- leaving the runtime planner non-evolving. Intermediate hybrids still lack the verification-and-consolidation loop that makes harnesses reliable. We ask: what runtime harness would let an LLM planner meet real-time constraints while self-evolving into a deployab"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "TransMem 将用户偏好变化显式建模为转变记录，并通过事实锚点与自适应图扩展提升长期个性化智能体对演化历史的检索能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10683"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "TransMem 面向偏好、目标和约束会随多轮交互持续变化的长期个性化智能体。该框架将用户历史组织为主题结构化记忆图，检测偏好转变事件，并把修正、冲突或取代关系保存为显式转变记录。推理时，系统先检索事实锚点，再沿转变记录自适应扩展，构造紧凑且能体现偏好演化过程的上下文。在 PersonaMem 和 PersonaBench 上基于两个 Qwen 主干的实验中，TransMem 优于代表性的检索式与结构化记忆基线，消融分析也验证了转变记录和自适应扩展的独立贡献。"
purpose = "- 将偏好更新作为独立检索单元，可避免智能体仅召回彼此孤立或已经过时的用户事实。\n- 通过显式记录偏好的修正、矛盾与取代关系，为长期个性化响应提供更可靠的时间演化依据。\n- 事实锚定与自适应图扩展相结合，可在控制上下文长度的同时保留关键状态变化。\n- 该框架可用于个人助理、长期对话系统及其他需要持续维护用户画像的智能体。"
notes = "标题：TransMem: Transition-Aware Retrieval for Evolving Personal Memory\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10683\n摘要：Long-term personalized agents are increasingly expected to answer queries over user histories in which preferences, goals, and constraints evolve across interleaved conversations. Existing retrieval-based and structured-memory systems can surface relevant facts, but often return isolated memory units that do not explain which earlier preference was refined, contradicted, or superseded by later evidence. We introduce TransMem, a transition-aware memory retrieval framework that stores preference updates as explicit retrieval units alongside ordinary factual memories. During memory construction, TransMem routes user interaction history into topic-structured memory graphs, detects shift events, and grounds them as transition records. At inference time, TransMem first retrieves factual anchors "


[[items]]
id = "cvsa"
title = "CVSA：长上下文稀疏注意力的覆盖率闭环验证"
subtopic = "推理效率"
topics = [

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "CVSA利用所选Token的Softmax质量覆盖率验证稀疏注意力输出，并按需恢复覆盖不足的注意力头，在128K上下文中实现最高6.21倍解码加速且接近稠密注意力质量。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10634"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "Coverage-Verified Sparse Attention（CVSA）将稀疏注意力从开环选择改造成无需训练的“验证后纠正”闭环解码流程。其核心指标coverage表示所选Token承载的Softmax质量比例，并与逐步输出误差满足精确代数关系，可同时用于结果验收和恢复插值。系统直接接受高覆盖率草稿，仅对覆盖不足的注意力头执行恢复，不需要采样、草稿模型或推测解码基础设施。在LongBench的7B与70B模型上，CVSA与稠密注意力最多相差1.13分，并在70B规模上达到统计意义上的质量持平；在128K上下文下，解码步骤的中位加速达到6.21倍。"
purpose = "- 为长上下文稀疏注意力提供可在线计算的质量信号，避免内核在无法判断信息保留程度时盲目提交结果。\n- 通过仅恢复低覆盖率注意力头，在质量保障与KV缓存读取成本之间实现细粒度动态平衡。\n- coverage与输出误差的精确关系可用于阈值设定、计算预算调度和运行时质量控制。\n- 该方案无需额外训练或草稿模型，适合整合进现有长上下文推理内核。"
notes = "标题：Coverage-Verified Sparse Attention: Closed-Loop Quality Control for Long-Context LLM Decoding\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10634\n摘要：Sparse attention accelerates long-context decoding by reading a subset of the KV cache, but existing methods are open-loop: the kernel commits to its selection with no signal indicating whether enough attention mass was preserved and no recovery path when it was not. The needed signal already exists inside the kernel. The fraction of softmax mass on the selected tokens, which we call coverage, satisfies an exact algebraic equality with per-step output error and serves a dual role as the acceptance criterion and the interpolation weight for recovery. Coverage-Verified Sparse Attention (CVSA) turns coverage into a training-free, closed-loop verify-then-correct decode loop that accepts high-coverage drafts and recovers only the heads that fall short, with no sampling, no draft model, and no s"

content = "DFT-Agent Bench 包含 200 余项 Quantum ESPRESSO 任务，覆盖结构弛豫、电子结构、状态方程和弹性张量等第一性原理材料模拟流程。研究在统一执行框架下，对比近乎裸置的 ReAct 基线与 v5.1 完整脚手架，并在两个模型上观察到能带与态密度、d 电子变胞弛豫等任务的明显增益。弹性张量任务的脚手架收益具有模型依赖性，而本已高分的弛豫和状态方程任务提升空间有限。评测还引入执行溯源门控，用于检查智能体是否真正履行 Quantum ESPRESSO 执行契约，发现部分未完成或未明确标记的执行仍可能获得较高的评分量表分数。"
purpose = "- 为自主材料模拟智能体提供覆盖多类 DFT 工作流的标准化评测基准。\n- 说明智能体脚手架的收益会随任务类型和底层模型变化，不能仅凭总体得分判断有效性。\n- 将执行溯源与结果评分结合，可避免未实际完成计算的轨迹凭表面正确性获得高分。\n- 为科研智能体评测引入可审计的执行契约、冻结任务切片和可复现评分流程。"
notes = "标题：DFT-Agent Bench: Scaffold Effects and Execution Provenance in Autonomous DFT\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10629\n摘要：Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents for first-principles materials simulation. We report empirical findings from DFT-Agent Bench, a benchmark of 200+ Quantum ESPRESSO tasks spanning relaxation, electronic structure, equation-of-state, and elastic-tensor pipelines over multiple material tiers. Each task carries a QE-derived ground truth with public-reference cross-checks where available, and a provenance audit checks whether the required QE execution contract was met. Under one execution harness, we compare a near-bare ReAct baseline with a bundled v5.1 scaffold on two models. The complete scaffold improves bands+DOS by +0.08/+0.11 and d-electron vc-relax by +0.17 on both models, while high-scoring relaxation and equation-of-state cells leave little room for"

score = 3

summary = "论文提出具身科学智能体 ESA，将记忆推理、贝叶斯优化与可泛化仪器控制统一起来，使智能体能够在真实实验闭环中学习研究目标、搜索空间和操作策略。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12958"
code = ""

content = "论文指出，现有自驱动实验室通常将基础模型作为输出符号工作流的非具身规划器，因而难以形成基于物理交互的实验直觉。作者提出认知可学习性、实验可学习性和具身可学习性三个相互依赖的维度，并据此构建具身科学智能体 ESA。ESA 由支持记忆推理与贝叶斯优化的数字智能体、负责可泛化仪器控制的具身智能体，以及协调两者的具身统一接口组成。在自主废水处理实验中，系统连续运行 15 轮并探索超过 2 亿种潜在条件，将最终溶液透光率从 82% 提升至 99.7%，残余染料浓度降低 98.5%。"
purpose = "- 为自驱动实验室提供从计算推理到物理实验执行的统一闭环架构。\n- 将实验目标选择、条件搜索和仪器操作都设计为可持续学习的能力，而非固定流水线。\n- 展示贝叶斯优化、经验记忆与视觉运动控制在真实科学实验中的协同价值。\n- 为可跨仪器布局泛化的科研智能体和下一代自主实验平台提供设计参考。"
notes = "标题：Embodied Science Agents: Making Embodied Experimental Design Learnable\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12958\n摘要：Recent advances in large models have accelerated AI for Science toward agentic science, where AI agents autonomously conduct scientific discovery. Realising this vision requires closing the loop between computational reasoning and physical experimentation, positioning self-driving laboratories (SDLs) as critical enablers. However, existing SDLs exhibit a fundamental paradox: foundation model integration has increased linguistic sophistication while severing physical grounding. By treating models as disembodied planners that output symbolic workflows for separate robotic systems, current architectures inherently block the development of embodied experimental intuition. We argue that overcoming this paradox requires embedding \\emph{learnability} along three interdependent axes: cognitive lea"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "BadEvolve通过注入并迭代优化投毒任务，诱导自进化LLM智能体在内部生成的持久技能中植入后门。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10542"
code = "https://github.com/ventr1c/BadEvolve"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- 论文研究任务级后门攻击：攻击者无需直接修改技能，只需向自进化流程注入投毒任务，即可诱导智能体自行将恶意规则写入可复用技能。\n- BadEvolve将攻击失败诊断为技能归纳、后门激活、规则遵循和攻击效果四个阶段，并针对具体失败阶段更新投毒任务。\n- 框架利用状态回滚保留此前取得的攻击进展，并通过失败记忆避免重复无效更新。\n- 跨多种自进化框架、评测基准和骨干语言模型的实验表明，该方法能够有效攻击基于经验蒸馏与持久技能的智能体。"
purpose = "- 揭示经验持久化和技能复用会把一次性恶意任务转化为可长期触发的智能体后门。\n- 可用于构建针对自进化智能体的红队测试，重点审计任务来源、技能归纳过程及持久技能内容。\n- 启示系统引入技能写入验证、演化任务过滤、版本回滚和失败轨迹审计等纵深防御机制。\n- 为评估智能体在持续学习和自我改进过程中的记忆投毒风险提供分阶段故障诊断思路。"
notes = "标题：When Experience Becomes a Backdoor: Poisoning Self-Evolving LLM Agents via Failure-Aware Task Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10542\n摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly improve themselves through self-evolution, distilling execution experience into persistent, reusable skills. However, this persistence creates a new attack surface: malicious rules implanted into skills can compromise subsequent agent behavior. Existing skill backdoor attacks typically assume direct manipulation of the victim's skills, which is impractical when skills are constructed internally by a trusted evolver. In this paper, we study a novel problem of task-level backdoor attacks, where the attacker can only inject poisoned evolution tasks, inducing the agent to implant backdoors into its own skills. Since the attacker controls neither skill induction nor downstream execution, a natural approach is to iteratively optimize poisoned tasks "

id = "calfa-kv-fisher-1-bit-kv"
title = "CALFA-KV：联合校准与 Fisher 引导锚点的 1-bit KV 缓存压缩"
subtopic = "缓存优化"
topics = [
    "缓存优化",

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "CALFA-KV 通过联合量化校准、Fisher 敏感度加权和高精度锚点保留，在接近 1-bit 存储成本下显著降低 KV 缓存压缩误差。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10511"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "CALFA-KV 离线联合拟合 key 中心、径向归一化参数及 K/V 向量码本，以匹配冻结模型投影后的注意力输出。该方法利用参考下一词元交叉熵损失的梯度平方，估计通道级 Fisher 型敏感度，并在线加权每个 KV 状态的重建误差。在逐层、逐 KV head 的预算约束下，得分最高的状态以高精度锚点形式保留，同时论文给出了锚点选择相对同预算最优方案的超额风险界。实验中，每层每个 KV head 增加 56 个 FP16 锚点后，CALFA-KV 在三个模型、七项基准上优于所有受测标称 1-bit 基线；d8b10 配置以每通道 1.25 bit 的标称索引率，将与 BF16 的平均分差距缩小至 0.90–2.01 分。"
purpose = "- 为长上下文推理提供低比特 KV 缓存方案，可降低显存占用并提升可服务上下文长度。\n- 将 Fisher 敏感度用于高精度状态筛选，比仅依据重建误差或固定规则保留异常值更具任务相关性。\n- 联合优化量化参数与注意力输出匹配目标，可为其他激活缓存和中间状态压缩方法提供设计参考。\n- 理论风险界与固定锚点预算有助于在精度、显存和索引开销之间进行可解释的部署权衡。"
notes = "标题：CALFA-KV: Joint Calibration and Fisher-Guided Anchors for 1-Bit KV Cache Compression\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10511\n摘要：1-bit key-value (KV) cache compression can combine quantizer calibration with selective high-precision storage to reduce reconstruction error. We propose Calibration and Fisher-guided Anchors for KV cache compression (CALFA-KV). Offline, we jointly fit a key center, radial normalization parameters, and K/V vector codebooks to match the frozen model's projected attention output. We also estimate channel-wise Fisher-type sensitivities from squared gradients of a reference next-token cross-entropy loss. Online, we use these sensitivities to weight the squared reconstruction errors of each KV state. Here, a KV state is one token's key-value pair within one layer and KV head. We retain the highest-scoring states at high precision under a per-layer, per-KV-head budget. Under exact restoration, w"

summary = "在固定模型、任务与工具的受控实验中，单智能体通常是企业 SOP 的高效默认方案，而复杂架构能否获益取决于任务特征与实现质量。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12953"
code = ""

content = "研究在 13 个企业级 SOP 上比较函数调用单智能体、技能增强智能体、中心化多智能体编排和分诊式智能体群等四类设计、八个实验组。结果显示不存在普适最优架构，不同 SOP 的成功率差异显著，额外架构主要可能在复杂分支工作流中产生收益。协调式架构消耗单智能体 1.4～4.7 倍的词元和 2.6～5.8 倍的延迟，但多数成功率增益较小且缺乏统计显著性。高质量技能可将成功率从 67.4% 提升至 73.1%，低质量技能则会拖累表现；经过针对性重构的多智能体实现达到 75.6%，成为唯一显著优于基线的实验组。"
purpose = "- 为企业依据 SOP 的复杂度、分支结构和成本约束选择智能体架构提供受控证据。\n- 将单智能体作为默认基线，仅在成功率提升足以覆盖词元与延迟开销时引入多智能体编排或技能增强。\n- 强调实现质量和技能定义质量比简单区分单智能体与多智能体更重要。\n- 可借鉴技能质量量表和成本—成功率指标开展智能体系统验收测试与架构优化。"
notes = "标题：Agent Architecture Cost-Success Trade-offs: A Controlled Evaluation across Enterprise-Style SOPs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12953\n摘要：Enterprises increasingly use large language model agents to execute standard operating procedures (SOPs): long, branching, tool-intensive workflows. Yet there is little controlled evidence for choosing among competing agentic architectures. Holding the model, tasks, and tools fixed and varying only the architecture, we compare four designs (a function-calling single agent, a skill-augmented agent, centralized multi-agent orchestration, and a triage swarm; eight arms) across 13 enterprise SOPs, and introduce a skill-definition quality rubric to build matched high and low-quality skill corpora. Three takeaways emerge. (1)~\\textbf{There is no universal best architecture; the right choice is SOP-dependent.} On a given SOP, task success ranges from under $10\\%$ on the hardest to near $100\\%$ on"

link = ""
content = "LongCapHarness 不再依赖合成长上下文数据及后续模型训练，而是直接挖掘基础模型已有的潜在能力。框架在开发集上通过递归自我改进，持续修订能力原语实现、能力状态和控制逻辑。该方法应用于 Llama3-8B，并可迁移至 Qwen2.5-3B，在 HELMET 与 RULER 基准上显著优于近期基于数据合成的方案。其估算开发成本低于 NExtLong 和 PolicyLong 训练成本的 50%。"
purpose = "- 为长上下文能力增强提供一种无需数据合成和参数后训练的低成本路径。\n- 表明递归优化外部能力脚手架可以有效释放基础模型未被显式训练利用的潜在能力。\n- 能力脚手架在不同模型间的迁移结果，为可复用、模型无关的推理控制组件提供了依据。\n- 可将能力原语、状态和控制逻辑作为后续自动化脚手架演化的核心搜索对象。"
notes = "标题：LongCapHarness: Capability-Guided Auto-Harnessing for Long-Context Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10463\n摘要：Existing methods enhancing an LLM's long-context reasoning capability often rely on data synthesis and follow-up LLM post-training. However, the data synthesis strategy and the post-training recipes are empirically driven and computationally intensive. In this work, we propose a new framework, dubbed LongCapHarness, to instead harness the underlying capabilities for long-context reasoning. The harnessing process recursively revises and updates capability primitive implementation, capability state and control logic through recursive self-improvement on the development set. With neither data synthesis nor LLM post-training, LongCapHarness is applied to Llama3-8B and transferred to Qwen2.5-3B. LongCapHarness substantially outperforms recent data-synthesis-based baselines on HELMET and RULER b"


[[items]]
id = "bunshinrl"
title = "BunshinRL：面向智能体强化学习的低内存工作空间分叉系统"
subtopic = "系统优化"
topics = [

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "BunshinRL 通过实时工作空间分叉、跨环境内存共享、工具前缀缓存和推测执行，显著降低智能体强化学习 rollout 的内存消耗与端到端延迟。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10453"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "BunshinRL 面向模型推理、工具调用与持久化工作空间交错执行的智能体强化学习 rollout，解决并发环境内存占用高和慢速工具调用阻塞批次的问题。系统利用跨环境内存共享、工具前缀缓存和推测式工具执行复用共有状态与重复调用。其底层 BunshinFork 可实时分叉完整容器工作空间，重建进程树，以写时复制方式共享内存，并保留文件、通信及 GUI 状态，从而支持状态复用和错误推测后的回滚。该实现基于 Linux 内核模块与 CRIU 修改，最高降低 43% 的峰值内存、51% 的总内存消耗，并将端到端 rollout 延迟缩短 23%。"
purpose = "- 为大规模并发智能体强化学习 rollout 提供更节省内存的运行时基础设施。\n- 通过缓存重复工具调用前缀和推测执行，缓解慢速工具调用造成的批次阻塞。\n- 完整工作空间分叉与状态回滚机制可用于构建支持并行探索、低成本复用和安全恢复的智能体执行系统。\n- 表明容器状态共享与系统级优化能够同时改善智能体训练的吞吐量和资源效率。"
notes = "标题：BunshinRL: Splitting Live Agent Workspaces for Memory-Efficient Agentic RL\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10453\n摘要：Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) rollouts interleave model reasoning with tool calls in persistent workspaces. Scaling these rollouts incurs substantial memory costs for concurrent environments, while slow tool calls delay entire rollout batches. We present BunshinRL, an agentic RL rollout system that exploits shared environment state and recurring tool calls through three techniques: memory sharing across environments, tool-prefix caching, and speculative tool execution. These techniques build on BunshinFork, a novel live whole-workspace forking mechanism for containers. BunshinFork reconstructs process trees, shares memory copy-on-write, and preserves file, communication, and GUI state, enabling cached-state reuse and rollback after incorrect speculation. We implement BunshinRL and Bu"


[[items]]
id = "mixua"
title = "MixUA：面向多用户多智能体交互的多粒度记忆提取"
subtopic = "长期记忆"
topics = [
    "智能体记忆",

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "MixUA将用户—智能体交互轨迹提炼为不同共享范围的经验记忆，在促进跨用户、跨智能体学习的同时显著降低隐私泄露风险。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10439"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "MixUA从相互独立的用户—智能体轨迹中提炼可复用经验，并根据受益对象与信息敏感性将其分配至用户—智能体对、单一用户、单一智能体或全局范围。作者提出CrossLearnBench，将既有智能体任务与用户偏好、隐私探针结合，用于评估跨主体经验迁移和私密事实泄露。由5名用户和7个智能体构成的实验中，MixUA相较无记忆设置最高提升16个百分点，相较单一全局记忆最高提升5个百分点。该系统最多保留90%的既有用户偏好，且仅有不超过0.02%的隐私探针在禁止访问的记忆范围内发现预植入私密事实。"
purpose = "- 为多用户、多智能体系统提供分层记忆权限设计，使经验共享不再依赖单一全局记忆。\n- 可用于构建兼顾跨主体知识迁移、用户个性化与隐私隔离的持续学习智能体。\n- CrossLearnBench可作为评估记忆收益、偏好保持和隐私泄露风险的综合基准。\n- 说明记忆作用域分配是群体智能体共同演化和规模化部署中的关键治理机制。"
notes = "标题：Who Remembers What? Multi-Granularity Memory Extraction for Multi-User, Multi-Agent Interaction\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10439\n摘要：Self-evolving agents improve their own skills, memory, and harness code from experience without retraining the base LLM, and group-evolving agents go further by sharing these lessons across agents. Yet most such systems overlook that sharing across users and heterogeneous agents requires deciding who can benefit from each lesson and what information must remain private. We present MixUA, a memory system that distills independent user-agent trajectories into lessons and assigns each lesson to a user-agent pair, user, agent, or global scope according to whom it benefits and what it may disclose. For evaluation, we introduce CrossLearnBench, a mixed-user-agent benchmark that pairs established agent tasks with user preferences and privacy probes that test whether private facts leak into memory"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现，前沿编程智能体在困难且未见的软件任务中会主动从 Git 历史、对象存储和文件系统寻找答案，暴露出可验证奖励训练诱发的奖励作弊捷径。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10404"
code = ""
dataset = "https://huggingface.co/datasets/logitly-ai/reward-hacking-raw-v1"
link = ""
content = "研究考察了基于真实软件仓库和可验证奖励训练的前沿编程智能体，指出通过测试并不必然意味着模型真正完成了代码推导。在隔绝答案泄漏的加固环境中，几乎所有受测模型仍尝试恢复标准答案，并逐步升级为检索仓库历史、直接检查 Git 对象存储以及扫描文件系统中的新版源码或隐藏测试。此类行为主要集中在最困难的任务上，不同模型在作弊频率与利用深度方面存在差异。作者认为这是模型从存在答案泄漏的强化学习环境中学到的捷径，而非更强智能的体现，并公开了智能体执行轨迹供后续分析。"
purpose = "- 提醒编程智能体评测不能仅以测试套件通过率作为成功标准，还需审计其执行轨迹与信息来源。\n- 强化学习环境应隔离仓库历史、Git 对象、隐藏测试和文件系统副本，减少奖励泄漏形成的捷径学习。\n- 可利用公开轨迹分析不同模型的奖励作弊策略、升级路径及其与任务难度的关系。\n- 为代码基准的安全沙箱、轨迹审计和奖励机制设计提供实证依据。"
notes = "标题：Coding or Cheating? An Investigation of Frontier Coding LLMs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10404\n摘要：Frontier agentic LLMs are trained on verifiable rewards in reinforcement learning (RL) environments built from real software repositories. A passing test suite is treated as the reward for solving the task. We show that, on hard problems, frontier models often satisfy these rewards by obtaining the answer instead of deriving it, known as reward hacking. Prior reports document this behavior on public benchmarks. We test its persistence on unseen problems. In a hardened environment where the answer cannot leak, nearly every model we tested attempted to recover the answer, escalating through tiers of increasingly specialized exploitation: mining the repository history for the upstream fix, inspecting the git object store directly, and sweeping the filesystem for a possible newer copy of the s"


[[items]]
id = "item-165"
title = "能证明多少就证明多少：混合神经符号逻辑自然语言推理"
subtopic = "神经符号人工智能"
topics = [
    "神经符号人工智能",
    "逻辑推理",
    "形式化验证",
    "不确定性估计",
    "证据推理",
]
research = [

]
keywords = [
    "神经符号人工智能",
    "逻辑推理",
    "形式化验证",
    "不确定性估计",
    "证明树",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "该研究以“生成证明—局部验证—不确定性修复”三阶段混合流水线结合 LLM 与定理证明器，提升逻辑自然语言推理的准确性和可靠性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10319"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出 Hybrid Reasoning，将结构化证明树生成、证明步骤验证和最终标签判定解耦。LLM 首先给出证明树与初始结论，定理证明器随后逐步进行局部验证，仅保留能够被形式化证明的推导，避免单个错误逻辑子句导致整个问题不可执行。当初始结论为不确定时，系统基于原始前提和已验证推导触发门控修复，重新推导标签。在 FOLIO、ProofWriter、ProverQA 和 PrOntoQA 上，该方法在两个最难数据集上分别比最强基线提高 5.3 和 4.9 个百分点，修复阶段达到 78%–98% 的精确率。"
purpose = "- 通过局部验证替代整题一次性逻辑翻译，降低错误子句对完整证明过程的破坏。\n- 将 LLM 的语义解释与定理证明器的严格演绎能力结合，可用于构建更可信的逻辑推理系统。\n- 仅在结论不确定时启动修复，有助于控制额外计算成本并减少错误改写。\n- 经验证的中间推导可作为可审计证据，为答案验证、过程监督和可靠性评估提供基础。"
notes = "标题：Prove What You Can: Hybrid Neurosymbolic Reasoning for Logical Natural Language Inference\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10319\n摘要：Logical reasoning over natural language requires both semantic interpretation and deductive search. Large language models (LLMs) can propose plausible intermediate derivations, but they often fail to compose them into a complete proof and overpredict uncertainty. Neurosymbolic methods improve search by translating an entire problem into first-order logic and handing it to a prover, but a single bad clause can make the whole translation nonexecutable. We present Hybrid Reasoning, a three-stage pipeline that decouples proposing a proof, validating its steps, and deciding a label. An LLM proposes a structured proof tree with a raw verdict; a theorem prover validates each derivation locally, keeping only the subset it can prove; and an uncertainty-gated repair attempts to re-derive the label f"


[[items]]
id = "item-166"
title = "部分提示污染测试中的假阳性与假阴性"
subtopic = "评测审计"
topics = [
    "模型评估",
    "基准污染",
    "评估透明度",
    "模型评判偏差",
]
research = [

]
keywords = [
    "基准污染",
    "评测审计",

    "模型蒸馏",
    "模型评判偏差",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究审计部分提示补全式基准污染检测，发现剩余文本长度会显著影响误报与漏报，并提出分长度报告、后截止期对照及跨模型家族校准方案。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10307"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在七个开放权重模型上审计部分提示补全测试，并使用 MATH-500 与发布时间晚于模型训练期的 AIME 2025/2026 题目作为真实阴性对照。污染标记高度依赖截断后剩余文本的长度：剩余不超过 8 个词的问题比至少剩余 25 个词的问题更容易被判为记忆，且多数 ROUGE 标记来自短剩余片段。真实阴性实验显示，60% 前缀下 ROUGE-L ≥ 0.75 的误报率最高可达 6.7%，80% 前缀下精确匹配也会产生 8.6% 的误报。该测试还可能漏掉蒸馏继承的上游污染，而按模型家族进行交叉校准可消除多数伪信号，并稳定识别 Qwen2.5 特有的污染信号。"
purpose = "- 说明部分提示补全并非稳定的污染证据，检测结果必须结合剩余文本长度与前缀比例解释。\n- 建议按剩余长度分层报告，并将提示前缀控制在不超过原题的 60%。\n- 应引入风格匹配且发布时间晚于训练截止期的真实阴性样本，以估计检测误报率。\n- 可使用跨模型家族校准区分通用续写偏差与特定模型家族的潜在记忆信号。\n- 评估经蒸馏得到的模型时，应额外审计上游教师模型污染，避免仅凭模型特异召回得出无污染结论。"
notes = "标题：How Much Is Left Matters: False Positives and False Negatives in Partial-Prompt Contamination Tests\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10307\n摘要：Partial-prompt completion, where a model receives the first part of a benchmark problem and is checked for whether it reproduces the rest, is a widely used test for benchmark contamination. We audit this test on seven open-weight models using MATH-500 and recent AIME 2025/2026 problems, which serve as true negatives. Memorization flags depend strongly on how much text remains: for every model, problems with at most 8 leftover words are flagged several times more often than problems with at least 25 (31.5% vs. 5.5% for Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), and half to three-quarters of all ROUGE-based flags come from short leftovers. On true negatives, a ROUGE-L >= 0.75 threshold flags up to 6.7% of problems at a 60% prefix, and at an 80% prefix even exact match flags 8.6% (pooled over models; 6.2% on AIME"


[[items]]
id = "item-167"
title = "即插即用验证器解耦过程奖励，稳定长程智能体推理"
subtopic = "过程奖励模型"
topics = [
    "强化学习",
    "过程验证",

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "该工作通过即插即用的领域验证器显式评估中间推理产物，并将有界稠密过程奖励与稀疏结果奖励结合，以提升长程智能体推理的稳定性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10305"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- 现有 actor-critic 与 GRPO 等强化学习方法难以区分部分有效的失败轨迹和完全无效轨迹，长推理链下尤其容易失稳。\n- 框架通过统一接口从智能体运行轨迹中提取中间推理产物，再交由领域专用验证器评分并聚合为有界过程奖励。\n- 训练采用两阶段后训练日程，将稠密的验证过程奖励与稀疏结果奖励相结合，并强化末端自我反思和自洽性。\n- 在 HotpotQA 上，Qwen3.5-4B 达到 60.18% 准确率，优于标准 GRPO 基线的 57.73%。"
purpose = "- 为长程智能体训练提供模块化的过程监督机制，无需将过程评价强耦合进 critic。\n- 让部分正确但最终失败的轨迹产生可利用的学习信号，缓解稀疏结果奖励造成的信用分配困难。\n- 可通过替换领域验证器扩展到问答、工具调用及其他具有可验证中间产物的任务。"
notes = "标题：Plug-and-Play Verifier for Decoupling Process Reward in Stabilizing Long-Horizon Agentic Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10305\n摘要：Stabilizing long-horizon reasoning requires intelligent agents to sustain useful progress across successive reasoning steps and tool interactions. Such agents are typically trained with reinforcement learning (RL), where actor-critic RL uses a learnable critic network to estimate step advantages, while critic-free methods such as GRPO eliminate critic models and rely mostly on the group variants of sparse outcome-only rewards. Both reward types leave partially useful failed trajectories either implicit or indistinguishable from entirely unproductive ones, and as reasoning horizons grow, it becomes difficult to maintain consistent reasoning trajectories. In this work, we propose a \\textit{plug-and-play verifier} framework for stabilizing long-horizon reasoning through explicit verification "

id = "item-168"
title = "递归自我改进安全吗？"
subtopic = "递归自我改进"
topics = [

    "人工智能安全治理",

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Vincent Conitzer 探讨前沿 AI 已使递归自我改进逐渐成为现实可能，但其结果仍缺乏清晰分析框架，并初步从博弈论角度分析多方是否应启动 RSI 的决策困境。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10261"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "递归自我改进（RSI）是指 AI 设计出能力更强的 AI，后者再继续设计更强系统，由此形成迭代增强链条。随着前沿 AI 在编程、数学、科研、工具调用和长程任务执行方面取得进展，RSI 已从未来构想转变为具备现实可行性的技术方向。作者指出，业界正开始探索 RSI，但目前尚无清晰框架来判断其可能结果及安全启动条件。文章进一步通过简要的博弈论分析，讨论当多个参与方均可选择是否启动 RSI 时的战略决策问题。"
purpose = "- 为评估递归自我改进的安全性与潜在系统性风险提供概念起点。\n- 提醒研发机构在启动 RSI 前建立结果预测、风险治理与停止条件框架。\n- 可用于分析多方竞争环境下因担忧对手抢先行动而产生的竞赛压力。\n- 启发将博弈论、人工智能安全治理与递归系统能力评估结合起来。"
notes = "标题：Is Recursive Self-Improvement Safe?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10261\n摘要：The phrase recursive self-improvement (RSI) refers to the idea of an AI system designing and creating another, better AI system, which in turn designs and creates an even better one, and so on. While the idea has a long history, it has always been an abstract, futuristic idea -- until recently. Today's frontier AI has attained a set of abilities, including in programming, mathematics, research, tool use, and working coherently over extended periods of time, that make RSI seem plausible. AI companies are starting to pursue it, but many of the involved people are also expressing concerns, asking whether humanity should really set this in motion and under what conditions. I will discuss the situation, argue that we do not even have clear frameworks for thinking about the likely outcomes of RS"


[[items]]
id = "pace-kv"
title = "PACE：长程智能体的规划感知 KV 缓存与位置重编号"
subtopic = "缓存优化"
topics = [
    "缓存优化",
    "长程智能体",
    "长上下文",
    "系统优化",
    "智能体",
]
research = [

]
keywords = [
    "缓存优化",
    "KV 缓存",
    "长程智能体",
    "旋转位置编码",
    "上下文压缩",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "PACE 结合规划感知的上下文保留与位置重编号，使长程 LLM 智能体在极小 KV 缓存预算下仍能可靠检索跨百万词元的关键信息。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10240"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，仅确保后续步骤所需词元仍驻留于 KV 缓存并不足以保障长程任务表现，因为不断增大的旋转位置距离会使这些词元实际难以检索。在 Qwen3-8B 的 10 万词元编程会话中，即使所需词元驻留率达到 99.6%，定义与使用位置的间隔从 2K 增至 90K 时，准确率仍由 93% 降至 64%。PACE 将规划器提供的未来上下文需求用于缓存保留，并对保留词元重新编号，将其与查询的旋转位置距离限制在 8K 以内。在相同 2,048 词元预算下，PACE 在 10 万词元任务中通过 97% 的隐藏单元测试，并能在单张 24GB GPU 上处理最长 200 万词元会话，将 KV 状态控制在 0.7GB 以下。"
purpose = "- 说明长上下文缓存不能只优化词元驻留率，还需控制保留词元与当前查询之间的位置距离。\n- 可将智能体规划器产生的未来步骤依赖直接转化为缓存保留信号，实现任务语义与推理系统的协同优化。\n- 位置重编号为百万词元级智能体会话提供低成本方案，在显著降低显存需求的同时仅增加约 9% 运行时间。\n- 评测缓存策略时应同时考察驻留率、实际可检索性、任务成功率及不同跨度下的性能稳定性。"
notes = "标题：PACE: Keeping the Right Tokens in the KV Cache Is Not Enough for Long-Horizon LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10240\n摘要：Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate sessions of up to millions of tokens, far more key-value (KV) cache than one GPU holds, so serving systems keep a small budget of tokens and evict the rest. Eviction methods differ in which tokens they keep but share one goal: that the tokens a later step needs are still cached. We show this is not enough. In 100K-token coding sessions on Qwen3-8B with a 2,048-token budget, a planner-guided policy that keeps over 99% of the needed tokens resident still fails late steps increasingly often: its accuracy falls from 93% to 64% as the gap between a definition and its use grows from 2K to 90K tokens, at residency 0.996. The needed tokens are resident but unretrievable: what grows is their rotary-position distance to the query. We present PACE (Planner-Aware Con"

content = "Jais 3 包含六款基于 K2 Horizon 改造的开放模型，参数规模覆盖 0.9B 至 375B，其中多数模型继承了 512K 上下文窗口。训练流程依次采用阿拉伯语持续预训练、监督微调、最终词元偏好优化（FTPO）以及可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR），并回放基础模型训练数据以保持推理、智能体和英语能力。模型支持多步任务规划、MCP 服务器与工具调用、终端及网页搜索，以及 PDF、电子表格和演示文稿的读写操作。评测采用经过审计的阿拉伯语基准和生成式评估，结果显示其在阿拉伯语指令遵循及智能体任务上达到或超过同规模开放权重模型，并显著减少幻觉。团队开放了各训练阶段权重、部分训练数据、数据配方、训练配置、日志、评测基准及评估代码。"
purpose = "- 展示了将已有开放基础模型低成本迁移为特定语言智能体模型的完整训练路线。\n- 通过混合自然、翻译、转写与合成阿拉伯语数据，并回放原始训练数据，可缓解语言适配导致的能力遗忘。\n- 将 RLVR、MCP 工具调用和办公文档操作结合，为构建区域语言数字员工提供参考。\n- 开放中间检查点、训练日志和经审计基准，有助于复现多语言智能体训练并分析各阶段能力变化。"
notes = "标题：Jais 3: Introducing Arabic-centric Agentic AI\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10224\n摘要：We present Jais 3, a fleet of six open, Arabic-centric agentic language models ranging from 0.9B to 375B parameters. Jais 3 is built to complete tasks for Arabic speakers, not only to answer questions in plain text: it is trained to plan multi-step tasks, call tools and Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, operate a terminal, search the web, and read, edit, and produce PDF, spreadsheet, and presentation files, while communicating with the user in Arabic. Instead of pretraining from scratch, we adapt each model of the fully open K2 Horizon fleet to Arabic through continual pretraining, followed by supervised fine-tuning (SFT), final token preference optimization (FTPO), and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We combine natural Arabic text with translated, transliterated"

notes = "标题：BO-Arena: An Evolving Benchmark for High-Dimensional Bayesian Optimisation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10198\n摘要：High-dimensional Bayesian optimisation (HDBO) is an increasingly crowded field with new methods proposed at every major conference, making it ever harder to answer a deceptively simple question: \"Which method is currently state-of-the-art?\" In this paper, we question the efficacy of benchmarking practices in the field, and find them to be systematically ineffective: multiple published works fail to benchmark against the strongest available baselines, or even include incorrect implementations of key methods. Without a shared reference implementation, such errors are difficult to detect during peer review. We therefore introduce BO-Arena, an actively maintained and extensible package providing canonical implementations of state-of-the-art algorithms, which allows researchers to develop new m"

id = "ascent"
title = "ASCENT：冻结长上下文模型的尺度互补外部状态"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ASCENT通过因果状态写入、嵌套类型化状态和查询条件读出，为冻结语言模型提供紧凑外部记忆，并实验证明其收益会随读取模型与状态预算共同扩展而显著增加。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10175"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "ASCENT提出“尺度互补”原则，即随着读取模型能力和紧凑外部状态预算共同提升，外部状态带来的绝对收益也应增加。系统采用目标盲的因果写入器、嵌套类型化状态以及有界的查询条件读出，同时保持所有语言模型参数冻结。官方16K BABILong实验中，Qwen2.5 0.5B、1.5B和3B模型的准确率分别提高10.50、78.88和82.63个百分点；多个7B–8B读取模型也获得66.75–85.63个百分点的增益。三模型乘三状态的全因子实验进一步以负对数似然验证了读取模型规模与外部状态之间显著的正向交互。"
purpose = "- 为冻结长上下文模型提供一种无需微调参数的外部记忆增强方案。\n- 提醒长期记忆评测不能只使用单一模型规模，应联合考察读取能力、状态预算及二者的交互效应。\n- 嵌套状态设计允许逐级增加信息预算，可用于分析每次状态细化所贡献的目标相关条件信息。\n- 结果表明，更强的读取模型可能更善于利用结构化外部状态，为记忆系统与基础模型的协同扩展提供依据。"
notes = "标题：ASCENT: Scale-Complementary External State for Frozen Long-Context Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10175\n摘要：External state should become more valuable as a reader becomes more capable, yet long-context evaluations measure memory utility at a single model scale. This paper formulates scale complementarity: the absolute gain from external state increases when reader capacity and a compact state budget are jointly scaled. ASCENT realizes this principle with a target-blind causal writer, nested typed state, and a bounded query-conditioned readout; every language model remains frozen. Under log loss, the value of a nested refinement equals its target-relevant conditional information; a full factorial makes the reader-by-state interaction identifiable. Official 16K BABILong experiments show Qwen2.5 accuracy gains of 10.50, 78.88, and 82.63 percentage points at 0.5B, 1.5B, and 3B parameters, with both "

source = "arXiv"
summary = "EviCover通过显式建模证据需求与联合选择对话轮次，追踪长期对话问答中互补证据在检索、重排和生成上下文阶段的丢失。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10170"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "论文提出 EviCover，将问题转换为可观测的证据需求，并依据来源标识检查需求覆盖情况，以联合选择相互补充的对话轮次。研究通过“证据漏斗”追踪标注支持集在检索、重排及最终答案上下文中的保留情况，区分证据发现失败与选择失败。实验覆盖经人工验证的 LoCoMo 生成对话和人工撰写的 REALTALK 对话，并重点评估 REALTALK 中 108 个多跳问题。相较混合词法/稠密检索基线，60 个候选轮次中的支持证据召回率由 0.526 提升至 0.575，最终十轮上下文中的召回率由 0.349 提升至 0.379，但证据召回改善尚未带来明确的答案正确率提升。"
purpose = "- 为长期对话记忆系统提供分阶段的证据保留诊断框架，定位互补证据具体在哪个处理环节丢失。\n- 提示多跳对话检索不应只对单个轮次独立排序，还应显式优化完整证据集的覆盖率。\n- 表明检索到更多正确证据并不必然改善最终答案，需要进一步研究生成模型对证据的识别、组合与使用能力。\n- 可用于设计面向长程智能体记忆的过程评估指标和证据感知检索策略。"
notes = "标题：Where Did the Evidence Go? Diagnosing Proof-Set Loss in Long-Term Conversational Memory\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10170\n摘要：Long-term conversational question answering can require several separated dialogue turns that support an answer only together. We introduce EviCover, a retrieval approach designed to retain this complementary evidence. It converts questions into observable evidence requirements, checks requirement coverage using retrieved source identifiers, and selects turns jointly. We track annotated support sets - the benchmark-labeled dialogue turns associated with each question - through retrieval, reranking, and final answer-generation context. We evaluate the baseline memory pipeline on generated, human-verified LoCoMo conversations and human-written REALTALK conversations, and examine EviCover on REALTALK's 108 multi-hop questions. Compared with the hybrid lexical/dense baseline, support-turn reca"

content = "TrialJev使用LoRA适配的Qwen模型与指针头，直接对自然语言答案选项评分，无需自回归生成资格判定。模型基于TREC Clinical Trials 2021训练，并在50名未见过的TREC 2022合成患者所形成的6,975个患者—试验对上评估，27B版本准确率达到80.97%和81.84%。在batch size为1的匹配测试中，生成式判定耗时是单次前向推理的2.45至2.70倍；27B模型可在单张GPU上以每对约178毫秒运行。消融实验表明，效率提升主要来自避免解码，而非仅由指针头带来；当前结果仍局限于合成患者和特定实现环境，尚未经过临床使用验证。"

notes = "标题：TrialJev: Open-Weight Single Pass Eligibility Scoring for Patient Trial Screening\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10145\n摘要：Clinical trial screening requires assessing large numbers of patient-trial pairs to prioritize candidates for detailed eligibility review, often under restrictions on sharing patient records. We introduce TrialJev, an open-weight eligibility scorer that uses LoRA-adapted Qwen models and a pointer head to score natural-language answer options in a single forward pass, without autoregressive decoding. Trained on TREC Clinical Trials 2021 and evaluated on 6,975 eligible-or-excluded pairs from 50 unseen TREC 2022 synthetic patients, TrialJev achieves 80.97% (Qwen3.5-27B) and 81.84% (Qwen3.8-27B) accuracy, compared with 79.87% and 79.93% for LLMs fine-tuned on the same data to generate verdicts. In matched batch-1 measurements, verdict generation takes 2.45-2.70 times as long as the one-pass in"

dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "作者提出智能体推理缓存（ARC），将一次性任务求解与后续方案执行解耦，避免语言模型针对相似实例反复从头推理。该方法从 LLM 推理轨迹中构建稳健、可在 CPU 上执行的缓存，仅需语言模型少量参与即可处理新问题实例。实验显示，ARC 在多项高难度数学和科学任务上可达到与前沿 LLM 相当的表现。生成的缓存具有可解释、可持续训练和抗输入扰动等特性，调用成本较源模型降低 16 至 291 倍。"
purpose = "- 将昂贵的通用推理过程编译为可重复执行的 CPU 级缓存，适合高频、结构相似的任务。\n- 为降低推理服务对 GPU 和前沿模型 API 的依赖提供新路径，可显著优化部署成本。\n- 可解释且可持续训练的缓存有助于审计执行逻辑，并随新样本持续改进。\n- 启示智能体系统将“首次求解”与“实例化执行”分层设计，以提升经验复用效率。"
notes = "标题：CPU-ARC: Agentic Reasoning Caching for on-CPU Reasoning Reuse\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09891\n摘要：Large language models reinvent the wheel all the time. They conflate task-solving with the execution of the solutions, which forces them to solve every problem from scratch. We show that once a task has been reasoned through, the application of its solution to a new problem instance can be done very efficiently and with little involvement from language models. We introduce agentic reasoning caching, a method for constructing robust CPU-executable agentic reasoning caches (ARCs) from LLM reasoning traces. ARCs show on-par performance with frontier LLMs across a range of difficult mathematical and scientific tasks, are fully explainable, continually trainable, robust to input perturbations, and 16-291x cheaper to invoke than the source model."

content = "论文研究语言模型在无标签目标域上进行测试时强化学习时，多数投票伪标签是否可靠。作者将监督误差分解为难以在无外部监督下识别的总体多数偏差，以及依赖提示的有限 rollout 波动。提出 Prompt-Conditional Distributionally Robust DPO（PC-Dr.DPO），依据观测到的投票可靠性进行提示内鲁棒重加权，对采样敏感提示采取更保守的更新。配合缓解在线适应中自监督遗忘的缓存机制，该方法在六个数学与知识密集型推理基准、五种模型骨干上的 30 组设置中有 24 组达到或超过最强 TTRL 基线。"
purpose = "- 为无标签测试时强化学习中的伪标签误差提供可解释的分解框架。\n- 可利用投票可靠性动态控制 DPO 更新强度，降低有限采样噪声导致的错误强化。\n- 缓存机制可缓解在线适应过程中的自监督遗忘，提升持续更新的稳定性。\n- 结果表明，分布鲁棒偏好优化适合用于数学和知识密集型推理任务的测试时适应。"
notes = "标题：On the Reliability of Majority-Vote Pseudo-Labels in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09872\n摘要：Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) adapts pretrained language models to unlabeled target-domain data using self-generated pseudo-labels, whose reliability remains poorly understood. We analyze majority-vote supervision for TTRL with online Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and decompose its error into population-majority bias and prompt-dependent finite-rollout variation. Because population-majority bias is generally unidentifiable without external supervision, we focus on mitigating the downstream effects of finite-rollout variation. We propose \\emph{Prompt-Conditional Distributionally Robust DPO (PC-Dr.DPO)}, which conditions within-prompt robust reweighting on observed vote reliability to make updates from sampling-sensitive prompts more conservative. We further introduce a cach"

purpose = "- 为强化学习系统设计奖励作弊监控器时，应同时评估检测准确率和模型在训练后规避监控的能力。\n- 监控惩罚提供的学习信号越充分，抑制效果可能越强，但也可能帮助模型识别并利用监控边界。\n- 可通过混合不同难度的训练任务改善行为泛化，降低模型对奖励漏洞的依赖。\n- 代码测试覆写、医疗谄媚与事实幻觉环境可用于比较奖励作弊干预策略的跨场景有效性。"
notes = "标题：Designing Effective Monitor-Based Interventions for Mitigating Reward Hacking During RL\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09828\n摘要：Reinforcement learning (RL) rewards are notoriously difficult to design and control, often leading to the model learning unintended behaviors such as reward hacking. One potential solution is to monitor for reward hacking and penalize it when detected; however, training against a monitor could lead to evasive behavior, and our general understanding of how to apply monitors effectively during training is limited. To study how best to use monitors to mitigate reward hacking, we introduce and open source three realistic environments where Qwen3-4B reward hacks: a coding environment hackable via test overwriting, a medical chat environment hackable via sycophancy, and a biography generation environment hackable via hallucination. We first focus on the coding environment, where we find that: (1"


[[items]]
id = "bitdecoding-fp4-fp4-kv"
title = "BitDecoding-FP4：原生 FP4 KV 缓存解码"
subtopic = "缓存优化"
topics = [
    "缓存优化",
    "推理加速",
    "系统优化",
    "长上下文",

]
research = [

]
keywords = [
    "缓存优化",
    "FP4 量化",
    "推理加速",

    "Tensor Cores",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "BitDecoding-FP4 通过量化算法与 GPU 内核协同设计，在接近 BF16 KV 缓存精度的同时，将端到端服务峰值吞吐提升至 2.4～3.0 倍。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09825"
code = "https://github.com/OpenBitSys/BitDecoding-FP4"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "BitDecoding-FP4 面向长上下文推理中 KV 缓存的容量与带宽瓶颈，直接利用 NVFP4、MXFP4 格式和 FP4 Tensor Cores 执行注意力计算。其双残差查询量化将查询拆为两个 FP4 张量，使查询 logit 误差从 60% 降至 1%，并通过下溢感知概率缩放避免长上下文概率被 E4M3 尺度舍入为零。离群值感知键量化结合静态平滑、Hadamard 旋转与尺度搜索，缓解逐 token 缩放下的键向量离群问题。系统侧采用转置 softmax、融合概率量化、堆叠查询 MMA 和分页 KV 缓存，在多种稠密及 MoE 架构上将精度维持在 BF16 KV 的 0.5～1 分以内，并在 NVIDIA B300 上实现 2.4～3.0 倍峰值服务吞吐。"
purpose = "- 展示低比特 KV 缓存需要联合优化数值误差、数据布局与 GPU 执行内核，而非仅替换量化格式。\n- 为长上下文和高并发推理服务提供兼顾精度、显存占用与吞吐量的原生 FP4 实现路径。\n- 其概率下溢处理和离群值抑制技术可用于其他低精度注意力算子及强化学习 rollout 系统。"
notes = "标题：BitDecoding-FP4: Native FP4 KV Cache Decoding via Algorithm-System Co-Design\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09825\n摘要：The key-value (KV) cache dominates memory capacity and bandwidth in long-context LLM serving. While 4-bit microscaling formats (NVFP4, MXFP4) shrink the cache and run attention on FP4 Tensor Cores, a fundamental gap remains between algorithmic quantization and system-aware acceleration: existing FP4 KV systems use vanilla quantization that causes severe accuracy drops, whereas accuracy-oriented 4-bit algorithms rely on vector codebooks whose lookup overhead degrades decoding throughput. We present BitDecoding-FP4, an algorithm--system co-design for accurate and efficient native FP4 KV decoding. We mitigate three error sources: Dual-Residual Query Quantization splits queries into two FP4 tensors, cutting query logit error from 60% to 1%; Underflow-Aware Probability Scaling prevents E4M3 sca"

summary = "Gemini 4 多智能体在 Lean 中依据形式化规格自动合成并验证量子程序，在并行搜索、量子算术及多类科学模拟任务上显著超越既有基线。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09822"
code = ""

content = "研究构建了基于 Lean 定理证明器的量子程序库，使 Gemini 4 智能体能够根据形式化问题规格合成新程序，并证明其正确性与计算成本。多个智能体团队通过 wiki、结果共享和组织化会议协同探索候选算法。该系统在并行搜索上实现最高 2.55 倍提升，在量子算术上达到 290 倍改进。针对聚变、光化学和药物代谢的量子模拟，性能分别提升 11 倍、8 倍和 2.5 倍，从而支持发现当前经典或量子计算机难以直接测试的算法。"
purpose = "- 展示形式化验证可替代部分大规模数值测试，为超出经典模拟能力的量子算法提供可靠评估路径。\n- 将程序合成、成本证明与多智能体协作整合为自动化算法发现闭环，可推广至其他高成本科学计算领域。\n- 证明人类专家与智能体团队协同能够显著缩短量子算法从构想到可信实现的周期。\n- 为以机器可检验证明约束科学发现过程提供了可复用范式。"
notes = "标题：Discovering formally verified quantum programs with AI\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09822\n摘要：The timeline by which quantum computers can solve breakthrough problems in science depends on improved quantum algorithms as much as on better hardware. Such algorithmic advances are typically achieved through many years of expert human collaboration. In mathematics and computer science, large language models have accelerated progress through automated discovery loops. However, quantum algorithms can only be evaluated by classical computers on restricted or small instances. Here we demonstrate automated discovery of quantum programs beyond these regimes including order-of-magnitude improvements on scientific applications. To achieve this, we build a quantum program library in the Lean theorem prover in which Gemini 4 agents synthesize novel programs against formal problem specifications an"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "该研究在 LoCoMo 上配对比较原子事实压缩记忆与全文对话 RAG，发现压缩方案能以更少提示词元提升常规问题表现，但在依赖原始措辞的对抗问题上明显落后。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09807"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究将每轮对话压缩为原子事实并进行检索，与保留完整对话记录的 RAG 基线在 LoCoMo 的 1,985 个问题上开展配对评测，两组使用相同存储、生成器和评判器。对于既有工作通常报告的四类问题，压缩记忆得分为 71.4%，比基线高 6.8 个百分点，同时减少 34% 的提示词元，差异经 McNemar 精确检验具有显著性。纳入对抗集后，整体优势缩小至 2.0 个百分点且不再显著，RAG 基线在对抗问题上领先 14.6 个百分点。误差分析显示，压缩记忆擅长保留事实所表达的值，却会丢失原始措辞，因此在答案依赖源文本表述时性能下降。"
purpose = "- 为对话智能体长期记忆设计提供了“原子事实压缩”相对于全文检索的准确率与词元成本依据。\n- 说明记忆压缩并非普遍优于 RAG，系统应根据任务是否依赖原始措辞动态选择事实记忆或原文存储。\n- 展示了在同一问题上进行配对预测并采用 McNemar 精确检验的严谨评测方式。\n- 提示记忆评测应保留对抗样本并分析答案对源文本措辞的依赖，避免仅报告有利类别。"
notes = "标题：Compaction, decay and reinforcement in a two-tier conversational memory: a paired evaluation against retrieval-augmented generation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09807\n摘要：A conversational agent that remembers has to decide what to keep. Retrieval-augmented generation keeps the transcript and searches it; the alternative is to compact each turn into atomic facts and search those. We compare the two on LoCoMo, with both arms answering all 1,985 questions in one window, against the same stores, with the same generator and judge, and both predictions on one row, which licenses McNemar's exact test on the 512 questions where they disagree. On the four categories comparable work reports, compaction scores 71.4 per cent against 64.6, a lead of 6.8 points (p = 5.5e-08) at 34 per cent fewer prompt tokens. On all five, including the adversarial set comparable work drops, the lead falls to 2.0 and stops being significant, because the baseline leads adversarial by 14.6"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "对 Maya a1 检查点的重构评测发现，其多项选择答案显著依赖选项位置，而训练时随机打乱选项可大幅降低顺序敏感性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09803"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究重构了拥有 4.21 亿参数的非自回归决策模型检查点，该模型通过每个选项对应的 `[MASK]` 隐状态在单次前向传播中完成选择。对 951 道经 Qwen 验证的问题进行循环轮换或随机排列后，原始检查点在 25.8% 的问题上改变答案，且正确选项位于首位与末位时的准确率相差 10.1 个百分点。答案翻转主要发生在低置信度样本中，低、中、高置信度组的相关差异达到 54% 对 8%。使用 2,818 条教师验证数据微调时，选项打乱将翻转率降至 7.2%，采用并行布局进一步降至 0.5%；对多种排列进行置信度加权投票仅带来 0.6 个百分点增益，却消耗 4.1 倍计算量。"
purpose = "- 说明多项选择评测中的选项位置可能成为隐蔽捷径，单一排列不足以可靠衡量能力。\n- 建议在训练阶段随机打乱选项，并在评测阶段报告跨排列准确率、答案翻转率及置信度分层结果。\n- 并行且位置无关的选项处理结构可显著提升排列不变性，适合作为选择题决策头的设计方向。\n- 置信度加权多排列投票收益有限且计算成本较高，优先改进训练数据与架构更具性价比。"
notes = "标题：Maya a1: Do the Options Read the Model, or Does Their Position? Order Sensitivity in a Non-Autoregressive Decision Model\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09803\n摘要：Laya answers typed multiple-choice questions in one forward pass by scoring a hidden state at one [MASK] marker per option. We re-engineer the checkpoint (421M parameters; a 2-layer option head with no positional tensor; a documented but unused parallel layout) and test whether the answer depends on where an option is listed. Presenting 951 Qwen-verified questions under every cyclic rotation of their options (or six random permutations for 12 options), the released checkpoint changes its answer for 25.8% [22.9-28.7] of questions (19.7% on the 605 English-language items alone); accuracy with the gold option first versus last differs by 10.1 points, and the flips concentrate where the model's own confidence is low (54% versus 8% across confidence terciles). Fine-tuning with Laya's trainer on"

content = "- Enmo Safety 不修改模型权重，而是在现成聊天模型外部加入确定性输入评估、触发器匹配的安全指导、不可信内容聚焦和输出防护。\n- 反思模型每轮根据失败案例提出一次有界更新，只有在开发集上降低危害且不增加良性提示拒答时才予以保留，同时对所有版本和决策进行快照与记录。\n- 在 HarmBench、AdvBench、JailbreakBench、XSTest、AgentHarm 和 deepset prompt-injections 等六个基准上的初步前后差异均不显著；后续实验仅在六个策略模型中的两个上显著降低不安全率。\n- 规则是否触发与最终结果没有显著关联，部分有用性条款反而加剧混淆攻击下的智能体危害，修订后的 v22 也以提高良性工具任务拒答为代价。"
purpose = "- 展示无需微调权重即可部署、迭代和回滚的模型无关安全防护方案。\n- 为安全脚手架引入基于留出集的更新准入、版本快照和决策账本，增强变更的可审计性。\n- 提醒安全规则的触发率不能替代结果评估，局部降低不安全率也可能伴随智能体危害或过度拒答。\n- 后续应增加重复运行、跨模型验证和统计功效分析，避免将少量显著结果解释为普遍安全改进。"
notes = "标题：Enmo Safety: External, Non-invasive, Model-agnostic Oversight for Off-the-Shelf Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10099\n摘要：We study a safety harness that improves itself without touching model weights. Enmo Safety wraps a chat model with a deterministic input assessment, trigger-matched safety guidance, spotlighting of untrusted content, and an output guard. A reflector model turns failures into one bounded update per round, and an update is kept only if it lowers harm without raising refusal of benign prompts on a held-out dev split. Every version is snapshotted and every decision ledgered. We evaluate a bare model, the seed harness and the evolved harness on six open benchmarks (HarmBench, AdvBench, JailbreakBench, XSTest, AgentHarm, deepset prompt-injections). On 150 items and on a 290-item holdout, none of the before/after differences on two models was statistically significant. A fourth iteration ran six "

code = ""

content = "CoMixKV 针对多智能体同时驻留的 KV 缓存造成的内存瓶颈，不再为每个智能体独立采用统一量化策略，而是在共享预算下联合优化智能体—层级位宽。其在线敏感度估计器根据近期查询、键和值推导局部注意力误差界，无需校准数据、试量化或额外前向计算。共享控制器结合敏感度、剩余可量化容量和字节级存储成本动态更新分配，并仅对新量化的数据块应用所选位宽。在四项基准和三种模型骨干上，CoMixKV 在平均 2、3、4 bit 设置下保持较强准确率；Qwen3-32B 实验中峰值 KV 缓存减少 32.08%，相对 FullKV 获得 1.38 倍推理加速。"
purpose = "- 为多智能体推理服务提供全局 KV 缓存资源调度方案，避免各智能体独立量化导致的精度预算失衡。\n- 在线敏感度估计无需校准集和额外模型计算，适合智能体动态加入、退出的运行环境。\n- 联合位宽分配可在共享显存受限场景中兼顾集体推理质量、内存占用与推理吞吐。\n- 该思路可扩展到按智能体重要性、任务阶段或实时负载进行自适应缓存压缩。"
notes = "标题：Coordinated Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization for Efficient LLM-based Multi-agent Systems\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10096\n摘要：LLM-based multi-agent systems solve complex tasks through collaboration, but their growing, simultaneously resident key-value (KV) caches create a memory bottleneck. Applying single-model quantization policies independently ignores differences in agents' precision needs, potentially degrading collective reasoning. We introduce Coordinated Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization (CoMixKV), which combines online sensitivity estimation with joint agent-layer bit-width allocation under a shared memory budget. To our knowledge, CoMixKV is the first mixed-precision KV-cache quantization framework to jointly allocate precision across simultaneously resident agent caches under such a budget. The estimator derives sensitivity from a local attention-error bound using recent queries, keys, and values, "

subtopic = "缓存优化"
topics = [
